```ts
// apps/vault/src/pages/words.ts
import type { PayRefusal } from '../pay.js';
import type { RailFailReason } from '../rails/rail.js';
import type { UnlockRow } from '../store/unlock-log.js';

export function moneyLabel(minor: number, currency: string): string {
  const whole = minor % 100 === 0;
  return new Intl.NumberFormat('en-IN', {
    style: 'currency',
    currency,
    minimumFractionDigits: whole ? 0 : 2,
    maximumFractionDigits: 2,
  }).format(minor / 100);
}

/** "Sun, 1 Nov, 11:59 pm" in India time, where the trips are planned. */
export function whenLabel(at: string | Date): string {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-IN', {
    timeZone: 'Asia/Kolkata',
    weekday: 'short',
    day: 'numeric',
    month: 'short',
    hour: 'numeric',
    minute: '2-digit',
  }).format(new Date(at));
}

const BRANDS: Record<string, string> = { visa: 'Visa', mastercard: 'Mastercard', amex: 'American Express', rupay: 'RuPay', discover: 'Discover', diners: 'Diners Club', jcb: 'JCB', unionpay: 'UnionPay' };

export function brandLabel(brand: string): string {
  return BRANDS[brand] ?? 'Card';
}

const CANT = "Go can't take this payment. Nothing was charged.";

export const PAY_MESSAGES: Record<'paid' | PayRefusal | RailFailReason, string> = {
  paid: 'Paid. Go is booking it now. You can close this page.',
  card_declined: 'Your bank declined the card. Nothing was charged.',
  needs_authentication: "Your bank wants an extra check this card can't do here. Nothing was charged.",
  rail_error: "The payment didn't go through. Nothing was charged. Ask Go to try again.",
  rail_not_ready: "This way of paying isn't ready yet. Nothing was charged.",
  link_invalid: "This link doesn't work. Tap Approve on the card in Go again.",
  link_used: 'This payment link was already used.',
  link_expired: 'This link has expired. Tap Approve on the card in Go again.',
  passkey_failed: "Your passkey didn't confirm it. Try again.",
  not_the_payer: 'Only the payer can approve this payment.',
  bad_approval: CANT,
  approval_expired: 'This approval ran out. Ask Go to set it up again.',
  wrong_audience: CANT,
  wrong_trip: CANT,
  used: 'This payment was already made.',
  currency_mismatch: CANT,
  over_amount: CANT,
  hold_expiring: 'The hold on this booking ran out. Ask Go to set it up again.',
  no_card: 'Add a card in your vault first: in Go, open You, then My vault.',
};

export function unlockLine(row: UnlockRow): string {
  const amount = row.amount_minor !== null && row.currency ? moneyLabel(row.amount_minor, row.currency) : '';
  const to = row.merchant ? ` to ${row.merchant}` : '';
  switch (row.kind) {
    case 'passkey_added':
      return 'Set up Face ID for your vault';
    case 'unlock':
      return 'Opened your vault';
    case 'card_added':
      return 'Added a card';
    case 'login_added':
      return 'Saved a login';
    case 'pay_attempt':
      return `Approved ${amount}${to}`;
    case 'pay_ok':
      return `Paid ${amount}${to}`;
    case 'pay_failed':
      return `A payment of ${amount}${to} didn't go through`;
    case 'pay_refused':
      return `Refused a payment of ${amount}${to}`;
  }
}
```

```ts
// apps/vault/src/pages/html.ts
const head = (title: string): string =>
  '<!doctype html><html lang="en"><head><meta charset="utf-8">' +
  '<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1, viewport-fit=cover">' +
  `<meta name="color-scheme" content="light"><title>${title}</title>` +
  '<link rel="stylesheet" href="/static/vault.css"></head>';

const mark = '<div class="mark" aria-label="Go">Go</div>';

export function homePage(): string {
  return `${head('Your Go vault')}<body data-page="home"><main>
<header class="top">${mark}<h1>Your vault</h1><p class="muted">Cards and logins, under your Face ID. The AI in your chat never sees them.</p></header>
<section id="loading" class="card"><p class="muted">Opening your vault</p></section>
<section id="expired" class="card" hidden><h2>This link has expired</h2><p class="muted">Open your vault again from Go: You, then My vault.</p></section>
<section id="register" class="card" hidden><h2>Set up Face ID</h2>
<p class="muted">Your vault opens only with your face or fingerprint. Nobody else in the group can open it.</p>
<button id="register-btn" class="primary">Set up Face ID</button><p id="register-msg" class="msg" role="status"></p></section>
<section id="unlock" class="card" hidden><h2>Unlock your vault</h2>
<button id="unlock-btn" class="primary">Unlock with Face ID</button><p id="unlock-msg" class="msg" role="status"></p></section>
<div id="open" hidden>
<section class="card"><h2>Cards</h2><ul id="cards" class="list"></ul><p id="cards-empty" class="muted">No card yet.</p>
<form id="card-form"><label for="card-element">Add a card</label><div id="card-element" class="field"></div>
<button class="primary" type="submit">Save card</button><p id="card-msg" class="msg" role="status"></p>
<p class="fine">Test cards only for now. Your card goes straight to the payment processor; the vault keeps only a token.</p></form></section>
<section class="card"><h2>Logins</h2><ul id="logins" class="list"></ul><p id="logins-empty" class="muted">No login yet.</p>
<form id="login-form"><label>Site<input name="site" autocomplete="off" required></label>
<label>Username<input name="username" autocomplete="off" required></label>
<label>Password<input name="password" type="password" autocomplete="new-password" required></label>
<button class="secondary" type="submit">Save login</button><p id="login-msg" class="msg" role="status"></p>
<p class="fine">Go keeps your logins safe here. It doesn't fill them in for you yet.</p></form></section>
<section class="card"><h2>Who opened your vault</h2><ul id="log" class="list small"></ul></section>
</div>
</main><script src="/static/webauthn.js"></script><script src="https://js.stripe.com/v3/"></script><script src="/static/vault.js"></script></body></html>`;
}

export function payPage(): string {
  return `${head('Approve payment')}<body data-page="pay"><main>
<header class="top">${mark}<h1>Approve payment</h1></header>
<section id="loading" class="card"><p class="muted">Loading the payment</p></section>
<section id="gone" class="card" hidden><h2 id="gone-title">This link has expired</h2><p id="gone-msg" class="muted"></p></section>
<section id="pay" class="card" hidden>
<p class="amount" id="amount"></p><p class="merchant" id="merchant"></p><p class="muted" id="purpose"></p>
<p class="refund" id="refund" hidden></p>
<div class="row"><span class="muted">Card</span><span id="card"></span></div>
<button id="approve-btn" class="primary big">Approve with Face ID</button><p id="pay-msg" class="msg" role="status"></p>
<p class="fine">The group agreed to this first. It works once, for this amount only.</p></section>
<section id="done" class="card done" hidden><div class="tick" aria-hidden="true">&#10003;</div><h2>Paid</h2>
<p class="muted">Go is booking it now. You can close this page.</p></section>
</main><script src="/static/webauthn.js"></script><script src="/static/vault.js"></script></body></html>`;
}
```

```ts
// apps/vault/src/pages/assets.ts
export const VAULT_CSS = `:root{--ink:#0A0A0A;--muted:#5F5F5A;--line:#E8E6E1;--soft:#F7F6F3;--good:#1F7A4D;--good-soft:#E8F5EE;--bad:#B42318;--radius:16px;color-scheme:light}
*{box-sizing:border-box}
html,body{margin:0;background:#FFFFFF;color:var(--ink)}
body{font:17px/1.45 -apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,"Segoe UI",Inter,Roboto,system-ui,sans-serif;-webkit-font-smoothing:antialiased}
main{max-width:440px;margin:0 auto;padding:28px 20px 48px}
.top{margin-bottom:20px}
.mark{display:inline-grid;place-items:center;width:36px;height:36px;border-radius:10px;background:var(--ink);color:#fff;font-weight:700;font-size:15px}
h1{font-size:28px;line-height:1.15;letter-spacing:-0.02em;margin:14px 0 6px}
h2{font-size:19px;letter-spacing:-0.01em;margin:0 0 10px}
.card{background:var(--soft);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:var(--radius);padding:18px;margin:14px 0}
.muted{color:var(--muted);margin:4px 0}
.fine{color:var(--muted);font-size:13px;margin:10px 0 0}
.amount{font-size:40px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:-0.03em;margin:0;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}
.merchant{font-weight:600;margin:6px 0 2px}
.refund{color:var(--good);background:var(--good-soft);border-radius:10px;padding:8px 10px;font-size:15px;margin:12px 0 0}
.row{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:12px;padding:14px 0;border-top:1px solid var(--line);margin-top:14px}
button{font:inherit;border:0;border-radius:14px;min-height:52px;width:100%;padding:0 18px;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer}
button.primary{background:var(--ink);color:#fff}
button.secondary{background:#fff;color:var(--ink);border:1px solid var(--line)}
button.big{min-height:58px;font-size:18px;margin-top:8px}
button:disabled{opacity:.5;cursor:default}
label{display:block;font-size:14px;color:var(--muted);margin:12px 0 6px}
input,.field{display:block;width:100%;margin-top:6px;padding:14px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:12px;background:#fff;font:inherit;color:var(--ink)}
form button{margin-top:14px}
.list{list-style:none;padding:0;margin:0 0 6px}
.list li{padding:10px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)}
.list li:last-child{border-bottom:0}
.small li{font-size:14px;color:var(--muted)}
.msg{min-height:1.4em;margin:10px 0 0;font-size:15px}
.msg[data-tone="bad"]{color:var(--bad)}
.msg[data-tone="good"]{color:var(--good)}
.done{text-align:center}
.tick{width:56px;height:56px;margin:4px auto 10px;border-radius:50%;display:grid;place-items:center;background:var(--good-soft);color:var(--good);font-size:28px;font-weight:700}
[hidden]{display:none!important}
`;

/** The one script both pages run. It reads the token from the address, never from the HTML. */
export const VAULT_JS = `(() => {
  'use strict';
  const parts = location.pathname.split('/');
  const base = '/' + parts[1] + '/' + parts[2];
  const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
  const show = (id, on = true) => { const el = $(id); if (el) el.hidden = !on; };
  const say = (id, text, tone) => { const el = $(id); if (!el) return; el.textContent = text || ''; el.dataset.tone = tone || ''; };
  const W = window.SimpleWebAuthnBrowser;
  const apple = /iPhone|iPad|iPod/.test(navigator.userAgent);
  const li = (text) => { const el = document.createElement('li'); el.textContent = text; return el; };

  async function api(path, body) {
    const init = body === undefined
      ? { credentials: 'same-origin' }
      : { method: 'POST', credentials: 'same-origin', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(body) };
    const res = await fetch(base + path, init);
    let data = {};
    try { data = await res.json(); } catch (e) { data = {}; }
    return { ok: res.ok, status: res.status, data };
  }

  let stripe = null;
  let cardField = null;

  async function home() {
    ['loading', 'expired', 'register', 'unlock', 'open'].forEach((id) => show(id, false));
    const r = await api('/state');
    const s = r.data.state;
    if (s === 'register') { show('register'); return; }
    if (s === 'unlock') { show('unlock'); return; }
    if (s !== 'open') { show('expired'); return; }
    show('open');
    $('cards').replaceChildren(...r.data.cards.map((c) => li(c.brand_label + ' ending ' + c.last4 + ', expires ' + c.expires)));
    show('cards-empty', r.data.cards.length === 0);
    $('logins').replaceChildren(...r.data.logins.map((l) => li(l.site)));
    show('logins-empty', r.data.logins.length === 0);
    $('log').replaceChildren(...r.data.log.map((e) => li(e.when + ': ' + e.text)));
    if (!stripe && window.Stripe) {
      stripe = window.Stripe(r.data.publishable_key);
      cardField = stripe.elements().create('card', { hidePostalCode: true });
      cardField.mount('#card-element');
    }
    if (r.data.start === 'add_card') $('card-form').scrollIntoView();
    if (r.data.start === 'add_login') $('login-form').scrollIntoView();
  }

  async function ceremony(kind, msgId) {
    say(msgId, '');
    const o = await api('/' + kind + '/options', {});
    if (!o.ok) { say(msgId, o.data.message || 'Could not start. Try again.', 'bad'); return; }
    let response;
    try {
      response = kind === 'register'
        ? await W.startRegistration({ optionsJSON: o.data.options })
        : await W.startAuthentication({ optionsJSON: o.data.options });
    } catch (e) { say(msgId, 'Cancelled. Try again.', 'bad'); return; }
    const v = await api('/' + kind + '/verify', { challenge_id: o.data.challenge_id, response });
    if (!v.ok) { say(msgId, v.data.message || "That didn't work. Try again.", 'bad'); return; }
    await home();
  }

  async function saveCard(ev) {
    ev.preventDefault();
    if (!stripe || !cardField) { say('card-msg', "Cards can't be added right now.", 'bad'); return; }
    say('card-msg', 'Saving');
    const setup = await api('/cards/setup', {});
    if (!setup.ok) { say('card-msg', setup.data.message || "Cards can't be added right now.", 'bad'); return; }
    const result = await stripe.confirmCardSetup(setup.data.client_secret, { payment_method: { card: cardField } });
    if (result.error) { say('card-msg', result.error.message || 'The card was not accepted.', 'bad'); return; }
    const saved = await api('/cards', { setup_intent_id: result.setupIntent.id });
    if (!saved.ok) { say('card-msg', saved.data.message || 'The card was not saved.', 'bad'); return; }
    cardField.clear();
    say('card-msg', 'Card saved.', 'good');
    await home();
  }

  async function saveLogin(ev) {
    ev.preventDefault();
    const form = ev.target;
    const r = await api('/logins', Object.fromEntries(new FormData(form)));
    if (!r.ok) { say('login-msg', r.data.message || 'The login was not saved.', 'bad'); return; }
    form.reset();
    say('login-msg', 'Login saved.', 'good');
    await home();
  }

  async function pay() {
    const r = await api('/info');
    show('loading', false);
    const d = r.data;
    if (d.state !== 'ready') { $('gone-title').textContent = d.title || 'This link has expired'; say('gone-msg', d.message); show('gone'); return; }
    $('amount').textContent = d.amount_label;
    $('merchant').textContent = d.merchant;
    $('purpose').textContent = d.purpose;
    if (d.refundable_label) { $('refund').textContent = d.refundable_label; show('refund'); }
    $('card').textContent = d.card_label;
    $('approve-btn').textContent = 'Approve with ' + (apple ? 'Face ID' : 'your passkey');
    show('pay');
  }

  async function approve() {
    const btn = $('approve-btn');
    btn.disabled = true;
    say('pay-msg', '');
    const o = await api('/options', {});
    if (!o.ok) { say('pay-msg', o.data.message, 'bad'); return; }
    let response;
    try { response = await W.startAuthentication({ optionsJSON: o.data.options }); }
    catch (e) { say('pay-msg', 'Cancelled. Tap Approve to try again.', 'bad'); btn.disabled = false; return; }
    say('pay-msg', 'Paying');
    try {
      const r = await api('/approve', { challenge_id: o.data.challenge_id, response });
      if (r.data.state === 'paid') { show('pay', false); show('done'); return; }
      say('pay-msg', r.data.message || "The payment didn't go through.", 'bad');
      if (r.data.retry) btn.disabled = false;
    } catch (e) {
      say('pay-msg', 'Something went wrong. Check the card in Go before trying again.', 'bad');
    }
  }

  if (document.body.dataset.page === 'home') {
    $('register-btn').addEventListener('click', () => ceremony('register', 'register-msg'));
    $('unlock-btn').addEventListener('click', () => ceremony('unlock', 'unlock-msg'));
    $('card-form').addEventListener('submit', saveCard);
    $('login-form').addEventListener('submit', saveLogin);
    home();
  } else {
    $('approve-btn').addEventListener('click', approve);
    pay();
  }
})();
`;
```

```ts
// apps/vault/src/pages/routes.ts
import { randomBytes } from 'node:crypto';
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { createRequire } from 'node:module';
import { dirname, join } from 'node:path';
import type { AuthenticationResponseJSON, RegistrationResponseJSON } from '@simplewebauthn/server';
import type { FastifyPluginAsync, FastifyReply, FastifyRequest } from 'fastify';
import { z } from 'zod';
import type { VaultDeps } from '../app.js';
import { masterKeyOf } from '../crypto/envelope.js';
import { readCookie, sessionCookie } from '../http/security.js';
import { findLink, hashToken, linkProblem, type LinkRow } from '../links.js';
import { authenticationOptions, finishRegistration, hasPasskey, passkeyConfig, registrationOptions, verifyAssertion } from '../passkeys.js';
import { approvePayment, payInfo, payOptions, type PayDeps } from '../pay.js';
import { listCards, saveCardFromSetup, startCardSetup } from '../store/cards.js';
import { LoginInput, listLogins, saveLogin } from '../store/logins.js';
import { personName } from '../store/people.js';
import { logUnlock, readUnlockLog } from '../store/unlock-log.js';
import { VAULT_CSS, VAULT_JS } from './assets.js';
import { homePage, payPage } from './html.js';
import { PAY_MESSAGES, brandLabel, moneyLabel, unlockLine, whenLabel } from './words.js';

const require = createRequire(import.meta.url);
const WEBAUTHN_JS = readFileSync(
  join(dirname(require.resolve('@simplewebauthn/browser')), '..', 'dist', 'bundle', 'index.umd.min.js'),
  'utf8',
);

const SESSION = 'vs';
const SESSION_SECONDS = 600;

const Ceremony = z.object({
  challenge_id: z.string().min(1).max(64),
  response: z.custom<Record<string, unknown>>((v) => typeof v === 'object' && v !== null && !Array.isArray(v)),
});
const CardBody = z.object({ setup_intent_id: z.string().regex(/^seti_[A-Za-z0-9_]{1,200}$/) });

type WithToken = { Params: { token: string } };
type TokenRequest = FastifyRequest<WithToken>;

export const pageRoutes: FastifyPluginAsync<{ deps: VaultDeps }> = async (app, { deps }) => {
  const db = deps.pool;
  const cfg = passkeyConfig(deps.env);
  const master = masterKeyOf(deps.env.VAULT_MASTER_KEY);
  const payDeps: PayDeps = { env: deps.env, pool: db, now: deps.now, rails: deps.rails, fetch: deps.fetch, log: app.log };

  app.get('/static/vault.css', async (_req, reply) => reply.type('text/css; charset=utf-8').send(VAULT_CSS));
  app.get('/static/vault.js', async (_req, reply) => reply.type('application/javascript; charset=utf-8').send(VAULT_JS));
  app.get('/static/webauthn.js', async (_req, reply) => reply.type('application/javascript; charset=utf-8').send(WEBAUTHN_JS));
  app.get('/l/:token', async (_req, reply) => reply.type('text/html; charset=utf-8').send(homePage()));
  app.get('/p/:token', async (_req, reply) => reply.type('text/html; charset=utf-8').send(payPage()));

  // ── home ──

  async function homeLink(req: TokenRequest): Promise<LinkRow | null> {
    const link = await findLink(db, req.params.token);
    if (linkProblem(link, deps.now()) || !link || link.kind !== 'home') return null;
    return link;
  }

  function hasSession(req: FastifyRequest, link: LinkRow): boolean {
    const value = readCookie(req, SESSION);
    if (!value || !link.session_hash || !link.session_until) return false;
    return hashToken(value).equals(link.session_hash) && link.session_until.getTime() > deps.now().getTime();
  }

  async function openSession(reply: FastifyReply, link: LinkRow, token: string): Promise<void> {
    const value = randomBytes(32).toString('base64url');
    await db.query('update links set session_hash = $2, session_until = $3 where link_id = $1', [
      link.link_id,
      hashToken(value),
      new Date(deps.now().getTime() + SESSION_SECONDS * 1000),
    ]);
    reply.header('set-cookie', sessionCookie(SESSION, value, `/l/${token}`, SESSION_SECONDS));
  }

  async function unlocked(req: TokenRequest, reply: FastifyReply): Promise<LinkRow | null> {
    const link = await homeLink(req);
    if (!link || !hasSession(req, link)) {
      await reply.code(401).send({ message: 'Unlock your vault first.' });
      return null;
    }
    return link;
  }

  app.get<WithToken>('/l/:token/state', async (req) => {
    const link = await homeLink(req);
    if (!link) return { state: 'expired' };
    if (!hasSession(req, link)) return { state: (await hasPasskey(db, link.person_id)) ? 'unlock' : 'register' };
    const [cards, logins, log] = await Promise.all([listCards(db, link.person_id), listLogins(db, link.person_id), readUnlockLog(db, link.person_id)]);
    return {
      state: 'open',
      start: link.start,
      publishable_key: deps.env.STRIPE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY,
      cards: cards.map((c) => ({ brand_label: brandLabel(c.brand), last4: c.last4, expires: `${String(c.exp_month).padStart(2, '0')}/${String(c.exp_year).slice(-2)}` })),
      logins: logins.map((l) => ({ site: l.site })),
      log: log.map((row) => ({ when: whenLabel(row.at), text: unlockLine(row) })),
    };
  });

  app.post<WithToken>('/l/:token/register/options', async (req, reply) => {
    const link = await homeLink(req);
    if (!link) return reply.code(410).send({ message: PAY_MESSAGES.link_expired });
    if (await hasPasskey(db, link.person_id)) return reply.code(409).send({ message: 'Your vault already has Face ID. Unlock it instead.' });
    const name = await personName(db, link.person_id);
    const { challengeId, options } = await registrationOptions(db, cfg, deps.now(), {
      personId: link.person_id,
      linkId: link.link_id,
      ...(name ? { displayName: name } : {}),
    });
    return { challenge_id: challengeId, options };
  });

  app.post<WithToken>('/l/:token/register/verify', async (req, reply) => {
    const link = await homeLink(req);
    const body = Ceremony.safeParse(req.body);
    if (!link || !body.success) return reply.code(400).send({ message: "That didn't work. Try again." });
    const done = await finishRegistration(db, cfg, deps.now(), {
      personId: link.person_id,
      linkId: link.link_id,
      challengeId: body.data.challenge_id,
      response: body.data.response as unknown as RegistrationResponseJSON,
    });
    if (!done.ok) return reply.code(400).send({ message: "That didn't work. Try again." });
    await logUnlock(db, deps.now(), { personId: link.person_id, kind: 'passkey_added' });
    await openSession(reply, link, req.params.token);
    return { ok: true };
  });

  app.post<WithToken>('/l/:token/unlock/options', async (req, reply) => {
    const link = await homeLink(req);
    if (!link) return reply.code(410).send({ message: PAY_MESSAGES.link_expired });
    const opts = await authenticationOptions(db, cfg, deps.now(), { personId: link.person_id, linkId: link.link_id, purpose: 'unlock' });
    if (!opts) return reply.code(409).send({ message: 'Set up Face ID first.' });
    return { challenge_id: opts.challengeId, options: opts.options };
  });

  app.post<WithToken>('/l/:token/unlock/verify', async (req, reply) => {
    const link = await homeLink(req);
    const body = Ceremony.safeParse(req.body);
    if (!link || !body.success) return reply.code(400).send({ message: "That didn't work. Try again." });
    const check = await verifyAssertion(db, cfg, deps.now(), {
      linkId: link.link_id,
      purpose: 'unlock',
      challengeId: body.data.challenge_id,
      response: body.data.response as unknown as AuthenticationResponseJSON,
    });
    if (!check.ok) return reply.code(400).send({ message: "That didn't work. Try again." });
    if (check.personId !== link.person_id) return reply.code(403).send({ message: 'Only the owner can open this vault.' });
    await logUnlock(db, deps.now(), { personId: link.person_id, kind: 'unlock' });
    await openSession(reply, link, req.params.token);
    return { ok: true };
  });

  app.post<WithToken>('/l/:token/cards/setup', async (req, reply) => {
    const link = await unlocked(req, reply);
    if (!link) return reply;
    const { clientSecret } = await startCardSetup(db, deps.stripe, link.person_id, deps.now());
    return { client_secret: clientSecret, publishable_key: deps.env.STRIPE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY };
  });

  app.post<WithToken>('/l/:token/cards', async (req, reply) => {
    const link = await unlocked(req, reply);
    if (!link) return reply;
    const body = CardBody.safeParse(req.body);
    if (!body.success) return reply.code(400).send({ message: 'The card was not saved.' });
    const saved = await saveCardFromSetup(db, deps.stripe, master, deps.now(), { personId: link.person_id, setupIntentId: body.data.setup_intent_id });
    if (!saved.ok) return reply.code(409).send({ message: 'The card was not saved. Try again.' });
    return { brand_label: brandLabel(saved.card.brand), last4: saved.card.last4 };
  });

  app.post<WithToken>('/l/:token/logins', async (req, reply) => {
    const link = await unlocked(req, reply);
    if (!link) return reply;
    const body = LoginInput.safeParse(req.body);
    if (!body.success) return reply.code(400).send({ message: 'Fill in the site, the username and the password.' });
    return saveLogin(db, master, deps.now(), link.person_id, body.data);
  });

  // ── pay ──

  app.get<WithToken>('/p/:token/info', async (req) => {
    const info = await payInfo(payDeps, req.params.token);
    if (info.state === 'used') return { state: 'used', title: 'Already used', message: PAY_MESSAGES.link_used };
    if (info.state === 'expired') return { state: 'expired', title: 'This link has expired', message: PAY_MESSAGES.link_expired };
    if (info.state === 'invalid') return { state: 'invalid', title: "This link doesn't work", message: PAY_MESSAGES.link_invalid };
    const labels = {
      amount_label: moneyLabel(info.amount_minor, info.currency),
      merchant: info.merchant,
      purpose: info.purpose,
      refundable_label: info.refundable_until ? `Free cancellation until ${whenLabel(info.refundable_until)}` : null,
      card_label: info.card ? `${brandLabel(info.card.brand)} ending ${info.card.last4}` : null,
    };
    if (info.state === 'no_passkey') return { state: 'no_passkey', title: 'Set up your vault first', message: PAY_MESSAGES.no_card, ...labels };
    if (info.state === 'no_card') return { state: 'no_card', title: 'Add a card first', message: PAY_MESSAGES.no_card, ...labels };
    return { state: 'ready', ...labels };
  });

  app.post<WithToken>('/p/:token/options', async (req, reply) => {
    const opts = await payOptions(payDeps, req.params.token);
    if (!opts.ok) return reply.code(409).send({ reason: opts.reason, message: PAY_MESSAGES[opts.reason] });
    return { challenge_id: opts.challengeId, options: opts.options };
  });

  app.post<WithToken>('/p/:token/approve', async (req, reply) => {
    const body = Ceremony.safeParse(req.body);
    if (!body.success) return reply.code(400).send({ state: 'refused', message: PAY_MESSAGES.passkey_failed, retry: true });
    const result = await approvePayment(payDeps, {
      token: req.params.token,
      challengeId: body.data.challenge_id,
      response: body.data.response as unknown as AuthenticationResponseJSON,
    });
    if (result.state === 'paid') return { state: 'paid', message: PAY_MESSAGES.paid };
    if (result.state === 'failed') return { state: 'failed', message: PAY_MESSAGES[result.reason], retry: false };
    return { state: 'refused', message: PAY_MESSAGES[result.reason], retry: result.reason === 'passkey_failed' };
  });
};
```

Modify `apps/vault/src/main.ts`: replace the whole file with this version, which also starts the sweep.

```ts
// apps/vault/src/main.ts
import { buildVault } from './app.js';
import { migrate } from './db/migrate.js';
import { makePool } from './db/pool.js';
import { readVaultEnv } from './env.js';
import { startSweep } from './go-callback.js';
import { makeRails } from './rails/index.js';
import { customerIdOf } from './store/people.js';
import { realStripe } from './stripe-client.js';

const env = readVaultEnv();
const pool = makePool(env.VAULT_DATABASE_URL);
await migrate(pool);
const stripe = realStripe(env.STRIPE_SECRET_KEY);
const rails = makeRails({ stripe, customerOf: (personId) => customerIdOf(pool, personId) });
const now = () => new Date();
const app = await buildVault({ env, pool, now, stripe, rails, fetch, logger: { level: 'info' } });
startSweep({ env, pool, now, rails, fetch, log: app.log });
await app.listen({ port: env.PORT, host: '0.0.0.0' });
```

```ts
// apps/vault/src/testing/inject-fetch.ts
import type { FastifyInstance } from 'fastify';

/** A fetch that answers from a Fastify app in the same process: no port, no network. */
export function injectFetch(app: FastifyInstance): typeof fetch {
  return (async (input: string | URL | Request, init?: RequestInit) => {
    const url = new URL(input instanceof Request ? input.url : String(input));
    const headers: Record<string, string> = {};
    new Headers(init?.headers).forEach((value, key) => {
      headers[key] = value;
    });
    const res = await app.inject({
      method: (init?.method ?? 'GET') as 'GET' | 'POST',
      url: url.pathname + url.search,
      headers,
      ...(typeof init?.body === 'string' ? { payload: init.body } : {}),
    });
    const out = new Headers();
    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(res.headers)) {
      if (value !== undefined) out.set(key, Array.isArray(value) ? value.join(', ') : String(value));
    }
    return new Response(res.body === '' ? null : res.body, { status: res.statusCode, headers: out });
  }) as typeof fetch;
}
```

```ts
// apps/vault/src/testing/start-test-vault.ts
import type { FastifyInstance } from 'fastify';
import type pg from 'pg';
import { buildVault } from '../app.js';
import { withVaultSchema } from '../db/test-db.js';
import type { VaultEnv } from '../env.js';
import { sweep } from '../go-callback.js';
import { FakeRail } from '../rails/fake-rail.js';
import { makeRails } from '../rails/index.js';
import { customerIdOf } from '../store/people.js';
import { FakeStripe } from './fake-stripe.js';
import { injectFetch } from './inject-fetch.js';
import { TestAuthenticator } from './test-authenticator.js';
import { signedInject, testVaultEnv } from './vault-test-env.js';

export interface TestVault {
  app: FastifyInstance;
  pool: pg.Pool;
  env: VaultEnv;
  rail: FakeRail;
  stripe: FakeStripe;
  /** Calls this vault in process. Give it to Go's vault client in tests. */
  fetch: typeof fetch;
  /** Where Go reaches the vault: a real port with `listen`, otherwise https://vault.test through `fetch`. */
  baseUrl: string;
  /** Sets up Face ID for a person through the vault page, and with `withCard` adds the 4242 test card. */
  passkeyFor(personId: string, o?: { withCard?: boolean }): Promise<TestAuthenticator>;
  /** Does what the pay page does: asks for the challenge, signs it with the passkey, approves. */
  approve(payUrl: string, who: TestAuthenticator): Promise<{ state: string; message: string }>;
  sweep(): Promise<void>;
  close(): Promise<void>;
}

/** A whole vault in this process: its own schema, a FakeRail, a FakeStripe, and Go reached through `goFetch`. */
export async function startTestVault(o: { goFetch: typeof fetch; now: () => Date; rail?: FakeRail; listen?: boolean }): Promise<TestVault> {
  const { pool, drop } = await withVaultSchema();
  const env = testVaultEnv();
  const stripe = new FakeStripe();
  const rail = o.rail ?? new FakeRail();
  const rails = makeRails({ stripe, customerOf: (p) => customerIdOf(pool, p), fake: rail });
  const app = await buildVault({ env, pool, now: o.now, stripe, rails, fetch: o.goFetch });
  let baseUrl = env.VAULT_PUBLIC_URL;
  if (o.listen) baseUrl = await app.listen({ port: 0, host: '127.0.0.1' });
  let second = 0;
  const signedAt = () => new Date(o.now().getTime() + 1000 * ++second);

  async function ceremonyCookie(path: string, kind: 'register' | 'unlock', auth: TestAuthenticator): Promise<string> {
    const opts = (await app.inject({ method: 'POST', url: `${path}/${kind}/options`, payload: {} })).json() as {
      challenge_id: string;
      options: never;
    };
    const response = kind === 'register' ? auth.register(opts.options) : auth.assert(opts.options);
    const res = await app.inject({ method: 'POST', url: `${path}/${kind}/verify`, payload: { challenge_id: opts.challenge_id, response } });
    if (res.statusCode !== 200) throw new Error(`${kind} failed: ${res.body}`);
    const cookie = res.headers['set-cookie'];
    return String(Array.isArray(cookie) ? cookie[0] : cookie).split(';')[0] as string;
  }

  return {
    app,
    pool,
    env,
    rail,
    stripe,
    fetch: injectFetch(app),
    baseUrl,
    async passkeyFor(personId, opts = {}) {
      const res = await signedInject(app, { method: 'POST', url: '/internal/links', body: { person_id: personId }, now: signedAt() });
      const path = new URL((res.json() as { url: string }).url).pathname;
      const auth = new TestAuthenticator(env.WEBAUTHN_RP_ID, env.WEBAUTHN_ORIGIN);
      const cookie = await ceremonyCookie(path, 'register', auth);
      if (opts.withCard) {
        const setup = (await app.inject({ method: 'POST', url: `${path}/cards/setup`, headers: { cookie }, payload: {} })).json() as {
          client_secret: string;
        };
        const setupIntentId = setup.client_secret.split('_secret_')[0] as string;
        stripe.completeSetup(setupIntentId);
        const saved = await app.inject({ method: 'POST', url: `${path}/cards`, headers: { cookie }, payload: { setup_intent_id: setupIntentId } });
        if (saved.statusCode !== 200) throw new Error(`card not saved: ${saved.body}`);
      }
      return auth;
    },
    async approve(payUrl, who) {
      const path = new URL(payUrl).pathname;
      const opts = await app.inject({ method: 'POST', url: `${path}/options`, payload: {} });
      if (opts.statusCode !== 200) return { state: 'refused', message: (opts.json() as { message: string }).message };
      const { challenge_id, options } = opts.json() as { challenge_id: string; options: never };
      const res = await app.inject({ method: 'POST', url: `${path}/approve`, payload: { challenge_id, response: who.assert(options) } });
      const body = res.json() as { state: string; message: string };
      return { state: body.state, message: body.message };
    },
    async sweep() {
      await sweep({ env, pool, now: o.now, rails, fetch: o.goFetch, log: app.log });
    },
    async close() {
      await app.close();
      await drop();
    },
  };
}
```

### Task 9: Go's payments table, the Pay interface and the signed vault client

Go keeps one `payments` row per ask to pay: the vote, the offer, the hold, the approval, the pay card and each finishing step. It holds no card data, ever. Go calls the vault only through `vaultClient`, signed with `VAULT_SHARED_SECRET` in the same format as the contract's `signRequest`, with a fresh nonce in every call so two identical calls in one second are never mistaken for a replay.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/brain/src/db/migrations/4000_money.sql`
- Modify: `apps/brain/src/money/pay.ts` (P1's type-only seam: keep its two members, add `withoutBothKeys`)
- Create: `apps/brain/src/money/ids.ts`
- Create: `apps/brain/src/money/store.ts`
- Create: `apps/brain/src/money/signed.ts`
- Create: `apps/brain/src/money/vault-client.ts`
- Test: `apps/brain/src/money/__tests__/store.test.ts`
- Test: `apps/brain/src/money/__tests__/vault-client.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `withSchema()` (P1, `db/test-db.ts`, returns `{ pool, schema, drop }`); P1's migrator; `Confirmed` (P1's seam `travel/travel.ts`, P4 owns it); `signRequest`, `verifyRequest` from `@go/contract`.
- Produces:
  - `interface Pay { askToPay; onPaid; withoutBothKeys(): Promise<{ count: number; paid: number; charged: number }> }` (master §6.3 plus one added member, in P1's seam file).
  - `rid(prefix, ...parts) => string` (a deterministic request or card id of at most 64 characters, e.g. `go_paid_<approval_id>_booking`); `paymentIdFor(tripId, spendRequestId) => string`.
  - `type PaymentState = 'asking' | 'waiting' | 'paid' | 'declined' | 'expired' | 'failed' | 'refused'`; `type ReleaseReason`; `interface PaymentRow`; `interface NewPayment`; `type PaymentPatch`; `insertAsking(db, p, now) => 'ok' | 'busy'`; `patchPayment(db, id, patch, now)`; `transition(db, id, from[], to, patch, now) => boolean`; `getPayment`; `byApproval(db, tripId, approvalId)`; `byCard(db, tripId, cardId)`; `openForDecision`; `paidForDecision`; `listForTrip(db, tripId, limit?)`; `bumpAttempt(db, id, error, now)`; `unsettled(db, limit?) => string[]`; `expireOverdue(db, now, graceMs?) => string[]`; `interruptStale(db, now, ageMs?) => string[]`; `paidApprovalIds(db) => string[]`.
  - `VAULT_SIGNATURE_HEADER`; `signVaultCall(secret, method, target, body, now)`; `checkVaultCall(pool, secret, req, body, now) => Promise<boolean>`; `keepRawJson(scope)`; `rawBodyOf(req)`.
  - `type PaymentKind = 'fake' | 'stripe_test' | 'ixigo'`; `type PayLinkRefusal`; `interface PayLinkInput`; `interface VaultClient { homeLink; payLink; bothKeys }`; `vaultClient({ baseUrl, secret, fetch?, now?, timeoutMs? })`.

```ts
import type pg from 'pg';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { withSchema } from '../../db/test-db.js';
import { paymentIdFor, rid } from '../ids.js';
import {
  expireOverdue,

let pool: pg.Pool;
let drop: () => Promise<void>;
const t0 = new Date('2026-10-05T09:00:00Z');

let asks = 0;
const ask = (over: Partial<NewPayment> = {}): NewPayment => ({
  payment_id: paymentIdFor('t_goa', `go_spend_test_${++asks}`),
  trip_id: 't_goa',
  decision_id: 'd_villa',
  option_ref: 'opt_villa_sol',
  purpose: 'Villa Sol, 3 nights, 6 to 9 Nov',
  label: 'Villa Sol',
  payer_person_id: 'p_rahul',
  payer_name: 'Rahul',
  for_person_ids: ['p_rahul', 'p_priya', 'p_arjun', 'p_meher', 'p_kabir'],
  hold_id: 'hold_1',
  amount_minor: 5420000,
  currency: 'INR',
  merchant: 'ixigo: Villa Sol, Anjuna',
  payment_kind: 'fake',
  hold_expires_at: new Date('2026-10-05T09:15:00Z'),
  refundable_until: '2026-11-01T18:29:59Z',
  ...over,
});

beforeAll(async () => {
  ({ pool, drop } = await withSchema());
});
afterAll(async () => {
  await drop();
});

describe('ids', () => {
  it('makes readable ids when they fit and stable hashed ids when they do not', () => {
    expect(rid('go_paid', 'ap_01JA7Q3V8Z', 'booking')).toBe('go_paid_ap_01JA7Q3V8Z_booking');
    const long = rid('go_paid', 'ap_'.padEnd(64, 'x'), 'booking');
    expect(long.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(64);
    expect(long).toBe(rid('go_paid', 'ap_'.padEnd(64, 'x'), 'booking'));
    expect(long.startsWith('go_paid_')).toBe(true);
    expect(paymentIdFor('t_goa', 'go_spend_1')).toBe(paymentIdFor('t_goa', 'go_spend_1'));
    expect(paymentIdFor('t_goa', 'go_spend_1')).not.toBe(paymentIdFor('t_goa', 'go_spend_2'));
  });
});

describe('payments', () => {
  it('keeps one open ask per vote', async () => {
    const first = ask();
    expect(await insertAsking(pool, first, t0)).toBe('ok');
    expect(await insertAsking(pool, ask(), t0)).toBe('busy');
    expect(await transition(pool, first.payment_id, ['asking'], 'waiting', { card_id: 'c_pay_1', card_state: 'waiting' }, t0)).toBe(true);
    expect(await insertAsking(pool, ask(), t0)).toBe('busy');
    expect((await openForDecision(pool, 't_goa', 'd_villa'))?.payment_id).toBe(first.payment_id);
    expect(await transition(pool, first.payment_id, ['waiting'], 'paid', { rail_ref: 'fake_ap_1' }, t0)).toBe(true);
    expect(await openForDecision(pool, 't_goa', 'd_villa')).toBeNull();
    expect(await insertAsking(pool, ask(), t0)).toBe('ok');
  });

  it('moves a payment only from the states it names', async () => {
    const p = ask({ decision_id: 'd_two' });
    await insertAsking(pool, p, t0);
    expect(await transition(pool, p.payment_id, ['waiting'], 'paid', {}, t0)).toBe(false);
    expect((await getPayment(pool, p.payment_id))?.state).toBe('asking');
  });

  it('patches named fields and the update time', async () => {
    const p = ask({ decision_id: 'd_three' });
    await insertAsking(pool, p, t0);
    const later = new Date(t0.getTime() + 5000);
    await patchPayment(pool, p.payment_id, { approval_id: 'ap_patch_001', confirmed: { ref: 'IXG-1' } as never }, later);
    const row = await getPayment(pool, p.payment_id);
    expect(row?.approval_id).toBe('ap_patch_001');
    expect(row?.confirmed).toEqual({ ref: 'IXG-1' });
    expect(row?.updated_at.toISOString()).toBe(later.toISOString());
  });

  it('expires a waiting payment only 120 seconds after its approval ran out', async () => {
    const p = ask({ decision_id: 'd_four' });
    await insertAsking(pool, p, t0);
    await transition(pool, p.payment_id, ['asking'], 'waiting', { approval_expires_at: new Date('2026-10-05T09:10:00Z') }, t0);
    expect(await expireOverdue(pool, new Date('2026-10-05T09:11:59Z'))).toEqual([]);
    expect(await expireOverdue(pool, new Date('2026-10-05T09:12:01Z'))).toEqual([p.payment_id]);
    expect(await getPayment(pool, p.payment_id)).toMatchObject({ state: 'expired', release_reason: 'expired' });
  });

  it('gives up on an ask stuck halfway after a minute', async () => {
    const p = ask({ decision_id: 'd_five' });
    await insertAsking(pool, p, t0);
    expect(await interruptStale(pool, new Date(t0.getTime() + 30_000))).toEqual([]);
    expect(await interruptStale(pool, new Date(t0.getTime() + 61_000))).toEqual([p.payment_id]);
    expect(await getPayment(pool, p.payment_id)).toMatchObject({ state: 'refused', refused_reason: 'interrupted' });
  });

  it('lists the payments whose room still needs work', async () => {
    const paid = ask({ decision_id: 'd_six' });
    await insertAsking(pool, paid, t0);
    await transition(pool, paid.payment_id, ['asking'], 'paid', { approval_id: 'ap_six_00001' }, t0);
    const ids = await unsettled(pool);
    expect(ids).toContain(paid.payment_id);
    await patchPayment(pool, paid.payment_id, { ticket_posted_at: t0 }, t0);
    expect(await unsettled(pool)).not.toContain(paid.payment_id);
  });

  it('has no column for card details', async () => {
    const r = await pool.query<{ column_name: string }>(
      "select column_name from information_schema.columns where table_name = 'payments' and table_schema = current_schema()",
    );
    expect(r.rows.map((x) => x.column_name).filter((n) => /pan|card_number|cvv|cvc|last4|password|payment_method/.test(n))).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/money/__tests__/vault-client.test.ts
import { signRequest, verifyRequest } from '@go/contract';
import type pg from 'pg';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { withSchema } from '../../db/test-db.js';
import { checkVaultCall } from '../signed.js';
import { vaultClient, type PayLinkInput } from '../vault-client.js';

const secret = 'test-only-vault-shared-secret-0123456789';
const now = new Date('2026-10-05T09:01:00Z');
const calls: Array<{ url: string; method: string; body: string; signature: string }> = [];
let answer: { status: number; body: unknown } = { status: 200, body: {} };

const fakeFetch = (async (input: string | URL | Request, init?: RequestInit) => {
  calls.push({
    url: String(input),
    method: init?.method ?? 'GET',
    body: typeof init?.body === 'string' ? init.body : '',
    signature: String(new Headers(init?.headers).get('x-vault-signature')),
  });
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(answer.body), { status: answer.status, headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' } });
}) as typeof fetch;

const client = vaultClient({ baseUrl: 'https://vault.test/', secret, fetch: fakeFetch, now: () => now });

const input: PayLinkInput = {
  approval: 'token',
  approvalId: 'ap_01JA7Q3V8Z',
  tripId: 't_goa',
  payerPersonId: 'p_rahul',
  payerName: 'Rahul',
  hold: {
    holdId: 'hold_1',
    amountMinor: 5420000,
    currency: 'INR',
    merchant: 'ixigo: Villa Sol, Anjuna',
    paymentKind: 'stripe_test',
    expiresAt: '2026-10-05T09:15:00Z',
  },
};

describe('vaultClient', () => {
  it('signs each call over its exact target and body, with a fresh nonce', async () => {
    answer = { status: 200, body: { url: 'https://vault.test/l/abc', expires_at: '2026-10-05T09:06:00.000Z' } };
    expect(await client.homeLink({ personId: 'p_rahul', start: 'add_card' })).toEqual({
      url: 'https://vault.test/l/abc',
      expiresAt: '2026-10-05T09:06:00.000Z',
    });
    await client.homeLink({ personId: 'p_rahul', start: 'add_card' });
    const [a, b] = calls.slice(-2);
    expect(a?.url).toBe('https://vault.test/internal/links');
    expect(JSON.parse(a?.body ?? '{}')).toMatchObject({ person_id: 'p_rahul', start: 'add_card' });
    expect(a?.body).not.toBe(b?.body);
    const check = verifyRequest(
      { method: 'POST', target: '/internal/links', body: a?.body ?? '' },
      a?.signature,
      secret,
      Math.floor(now.getTime() / 1000),
    );
    expect(check).toEqual({ ok: true });
  });

  it('sends the approval and the hold in the vault words', async () => {
    answer = { status: 200, body: { url: 'https://vault.test/p/xyz', expires_at: '2026-10-05T09:06:00.000Z' } };
    expect(await client.payLink(input)).toEqual({ ok: true, url: 'https://vault.test/p/xyz', expiresAt: '2026-10-05T09:06:00.000Z' });
    expect(JSON.parse(calls.at(-1)?.body ?? '{}')).toMatchObject({
      approval: 'token',
      approval_id: 'ap_01JA7Q3V8Z',
      trip_id: 't_goa',
      payer_person_id: 'p_rahul',
      payer_name: 'Rahul',
      hold: { hold_id: 'hold_1', amount_minor: 5420000, currency: 'INR', merchant: 'ixigo: Villa Sol, Anjuna', payment_kind: 'stripe_test', expires_at: '2026-10-05T09:15:00Z' },
    });
  });

  it('turns a vault refusal into its reason, and anything else into unavailable', async () => {
    answer = { status: 409, body: { error: { code: 'conflict', message: 'x', reason: 'over_amount' } } };
    expect(await client.payLink(input)).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: 'over_amount' });
    answer = { status: 500, body: {} };
    expect(await client.payLink(input)).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: 'unavailable' });
  });

  it('gives up on a vault that does not answer in time', async () => {
    const hanging = ((_input: string | URL | Request, init?: RequestInit) =>
      new Promise<Response>((_resolve, reject) => {
        init?.signal?.addEventListener('abort', () => reject(new Error('aborted')));
      })) as typeof fetch;
    const slow = vaultClient({ baseUrl: 'https://vault.test', secret, fetch: hanging, now: () => now });
    expect(await slow.payLink(input, { timeoutMs: 50 })).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: 'unavailable' });
  });

  it('reads the both-keys count with a signed GET', async () => {
    answer = { status: 200, body: { charged_approval_ids: ['ap_1'], without_both_keys: 0 } };
    expect(await client.bothKeys()).toEqual({ chargedApprovalIds: ['ap_1'], withoutBothKeys: 0 });
    const call = calls.at(-1);
    const target = new URL(call?.url ?? '').pathname + new URL(call?.url ?? '').search;
    expect(target).toMatch(/^\/internal\/both-keys\?n=[0-9a-f]{16}$/);
    expect(verifyRequest({ method: 'GET', target, body: '' }, call?.signature, secret, Math.floor(now.getTime() / 1000))).toEqual({ ok: true });
  });
});

describe('checkVaultCall', () => {
  let pool: pg.Pool;
  let drop: () => Promise<void>;
  beforeAll(async () => {
    ({ pool, drop } = await withSchema());
  });
  afterAll(async () => {
    await drop();
  });

  const body = JSON.stringify({ trip_id: 't_goa', approval_id: 'ap_1', rail_ref: 'fake_ap_1' });
  const req = (signature: string) => ({ method: 'POST', url: '/internal/vault/paid', headers: { 'x-vault-signature': signature } });

  it('accepts a good signature once', async () => {
    const sig = signRequest({ method: 'POST', target: '/internal/vault/paid', body }, secret, Math.floor(now.getTime() / 1000));
    expect(await checkVaultCall(pool, secret, req(sig), body, now)).toBe(true);
    expect(await checkVaultCall(pool, secret, req(sig), body, now)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('refuses another secret, another body and a stale time', async () => {
    const t = Math.floor(now.getTime() / 1000);
    expect(await checkVaultCall(pool, secret, req(signRequest({ method: 'POST', target: '/internal/vault/paid', body }, 'x'.repeat(40), t)), body, now)).toBe(false);
    expect(await checkVaultCall(pool, secret, req(signRequest({ method: 'POST', target: '/internal/vault/paid', body: '{}' }, secret, t)), body, now)).toBe(false);
    expect(await checkVaultCall(pool, secret, req(signRequest({ method: 'POST', target: '/internal/vault/paid', body }, secret, t - 400)), body, now)).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

```sql
-- apps/brain/src/db/migrations/4000_money.sql
-- Go's record of each ask to pay. It holds no card data, ever: only ids, amounts and states.
create table payments (
  payment_id text primary key,
  trip_id text not null,
  decision_id text not null,
  -- The winning vote option's option_ref (an options card's option_id), as the vote names it. A fresh ask
  -- resolves it to the travel offer again with Travel.offer; it is never itself a travel offer id.
  option_ref text not null,
  purpose text not null,
  -- The voted option's name, for Go's lines ("Villa Sol").
  label text not null,
  payer_person_id text not null,
  payer_name text not null,
  for_person_ids text[] not null,
  state text not null check (state in ('asking', 'waiting', 'paid', 'declined', 'expired', 'failed', 'refused')),
  refused_reason text,
  release_reason text check (release_reason in ('payment_failed', 'payer_declined', 'price_changed', 'expired', 'other')),
  hold_id text,
  amount_minor integer,
  currency text,
  merchant text,
  payment_kind text,
  hold_expires_at timestamptz,
  -- Kept exactly as the travel layer gave it, because it is signed into the approval.
  refundable_until text,
  approval_id text unique,
  approval_token text,
  approval_expires_at timestamptz,
  card_id text unique,
  card_state text,
  pay_link_url text,
  pay_link_expires_at timestamptz,
  rail_ref text,
  fail_reason text,
  confirmed jsonb,
  booking_id text,
  expense_id text,
  released_at timestamptz,
  ticket_posted_at timestamptz,
  said_at timestamptz,
  attempts integer not null default 0,
  last_error text,
  created_at timestamptz not null,
  updated_at timestamptz not null
);
-- One open ask per vote, so two paths can never post two pay cards.
create unique index payments_one_open on payments (trip_id, decision_id) where state in ('asking', 'waiting');
create index payments_trip on payments (trip_id, created_at desc);

-- Signatures on the vault's calls back to Go, each accepted once.
create table vault_callback_seen (
  t bigint not null,
  v1 text not null,
  seen_at timestamptz not null,
  primary key (t, v1)
);
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/money/pay.ts
/**
 * The only path to spending. Flow: hold → request_spend_approval → pay card → the payer taps Approve →
 * the vault page → passkey → the vault checks the approval and pays the hold's rail → the vault calls Go
 * back → confirm → add_booking (with approval_id) → add_expense → cards. Any failure: cancel_spend_approval
 * and say so. Neither key is ever the model's word.
 */
export interface Pay {
  askToPay(tripId: string, o: { decisionId: string; offerId: string; purpose: string }): Promise<{ cardId: string } | { refused: string }>;
  /** apps/vault calls POST /internal/vault/paid (signed with VAULT_SHARED_SECRET); P5 finishes the booking. */
  onPaid(fn: (p: { tripId: string; approvalId: string; railRef: string }) => Promise<void>): void;
  /** Payments that went through without both keys, counted from the vault's unlock log and Go's payments. Always 0. */
  withoutBothKeys(): Promise<{ count: number; paid: number; charged: number }>;
}
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/money/store.ts
import type pg from 'pg';
import type { Confirmed } from '../travel/travel.js';

export type PaymentState = 'asking' | 'waiting' | 'paid' | 'declined' | 'expired' | 'failed' | 'refused';
export type ReleaseReason = 'payment_failed' | 'payer_declined' | 'price_changed' | 'expired' | 'other';
type Db = pg.Pool | pg.PoolClient;

export interface PaymentRow {
  payment_id: string;
  trip_id: string;
  decision_id: string;
  option_ref: string;
  purpose: string;
  label: string;
  payer_person_id: string;
  payer_name: string;
  for_person_ids: string[];
  state: PaymentState;
  refused_reason: string | null;
  release_reason: ReleaseReason | null;
  hold_id: string | null;
  amount_minor: number | null;
  currency: string | null;
  merchant: string | null;
  payment_kind: string | null;
  hold_expires_at: Date | null;
  refundable_until: string | null;
  approval_id: string | null;
  approval_token: string | null;
  approval_expires_at: Date | null;
  card_id: string | null;
  card_state: string | null;
  pay_link_url: string | null;
  pay_link_expires_at: Date | null;
  rail_ref: string | null;
  fail_reason: string | null;
  confirmed: Confirmed | null;
  booking_id: string | null;
  expense_id: string | null;
  released_at: Date | null;
  ticket_posted_at: Date | null;
  said_at: Date | null;
  attempts: number;
  last_error: string | null;
  created_at: Date;
  updated_at: Date;
}

export interface NewPayment {
  payment_id: string;
  trip_id: string;
  decision_id: string;
  option_ref: string;
  purpose: string;
  label: string;
  payer_person_id: string;
  payer_name: string;
  for_person_ids: string[];
  hold_id: string;
  amount_minor: number;
  currency: string;
  merchant: string;
  payment_kind: string;
  hold_expires_at: Date;
  refundable_until: string | null;
}

const PATCHABLE = [
  'refused_reason', 'release_reason', 'approval_id', 'approval_token', 'approval_expires_at', 'card_id', 'card_state',
  'pay_link_url', 'pay_link_expires_at', 'rail_ref', 'fail_reason', 'confirmed', 'booking_id', 'expense_id',
  'released_at', 'ticket_posted_at', 'said_at', 'last_error',
] as const;
export type PaymentPatch = Partial<Pick<PaymentRow, (typeof PATCHABLE)[number]>>;

/** "set a = $n, b = $n+1" for the known keys in the patch, and their values. */
function setClause(patch: PaymentPatch, first: number): { sql: string[]; values: unknown[] } {
  const sql: string[] = [];
  const values: unknown[] = [];
  for (const key of PATCHABLE) {
    if (!(key in patch)) continue;
    const value = patch[key];
    sql.push(`${key} = $${first + values.length}`);
    values.push(key === 'confirmed' && value !== null && value !== undefined ? JSON.stringify(value) : value);
  }
  return { sql, values };
}

export async function insertAsking(db: Db, p: NewPayment, now: Date): Promise<'ok' | 'busy'> {
  try {
    await db.query(
      `insert into payments (payment_id, trip_id, decision_id, option_ref, purpose, label, payer_person_id, payer_name, for_person_ids, state,
         hold_id, amount_minor, currency, merchant, payment_kind, hold_expires_at, refundable_until, created_at, updated_at)
       values ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $7, $8, $9, 'asking', $10, $11, $12, $13, $14, $15, $16, $17, $17)`,
      [
        p.payment_id, p.trip_id, p.decision_id, p.option_ref, p.purpose, p.label, p.payer_person_id, p.payer_name, p.for_person_ids,
        p.hold_id, p.amount_minor, p.currency, p.merchant, p.payment_kind, p.hold_expires_at, p.refundable_until, now,
      ],
    );
    return 'ok';
  } catch (e) {
    if ((e as { code?: string }).code === '23505') return 'busy';
    throw e;
  }
}

export async function patchPayment(db: Db, id: string, patch: PaymentPatch, now: Date): Promise<void> {
  const set = setClause(patch, 3);
  await db.query(`update payments set ${[...set.sql, 'updated_at = $2'].join(', ')} where payment_id = $1`, [id, now, ...set.values]);
}

/** Moves a payment to `to` only from one of `from`. False when another path moved it first. */
export async function transition(db: Db, id: string, from: PaymentState[], to: PaymentState, patch: PaymentPatch, now: Date): Promise<boolean> {
  const set = setClause(patch, 5);
  const r = await db.query(
    `update payments set ${['state = $3', 'updated_at = $2', ...set.sql].join(', ')} where payment_id = $1 and state = any($4::text[])`,
    [id, now, to, from, ...set.values],
  );
  return (r.rowCount ?? 0) > 0;
}

async function one(db: Db, where: string, values: unknown[]): Promise<PaymentRow | null> {
  const r = await db.query<PaymentRow>(`select * from payments where ${where} order by created_at desc limit 1`, values);
  return r.rows[0] ?? null;
}

export const getPayment = (db: Db, id: string) => one(db, 'payment_id = $1', [id]);
export const byApproval = (db: Db, tripId: string, approvalId: string) => one(db, 'trip_id = $1 and approval_id = $2', [tripId, approvalId]);
export const byCard = (db: Db, tripId: string, cardId: string) => one(db, 'trip_id = $1 and card_id = $2', [tripId, cardId]);
export const openForDecision = (db: Db, tripId: string, decisionId: string) =>
  one(db, "trip_id = $1 and decision_id = $2 and state in ('asking', 'waiting')", [tripId, decisionId]);
export const paidForDecision = (db: Db, tripId: string, decisionId: string) =>
  one(db, "trip_id = $1 and decision_id = $2 and state = 'paid'", [tripId, decisionId]);

export async function listForTrip(db: Db, tripId: string, limit = 12): Promise<PaymentRow[]> {
  const r = await db.query<PaymentRow>('select * from payments where trip_id = $1 order by created_at desc limit $2', [tripId, limit]);
  return r.rows;
}

export async function bumpAttempt(db: Db, id: string, error: string, now: Date): Promise<void> {
  await db.query('update payments set attempts = attempts + 1, last_error = $2, updated_at = $3 where payment_id = $1', [id, error.slice(0, 300), now]);
}

/** Payments whose room or Server still needs a step: a release, a card update, a booking, a line. */
export async function unsettled(db: Db, limit = 50): Promise<string[]> {
  const r = await db.query<{ payment_id: string }>(
    `select payment_id from payments where attempts < 20 and (
       (state = 'paid' and ticket_posted_at is null)
       or (state in ('declined', 'expired', 'failed', 'refused') and approval_id is not null and released_at is null)
       or (state in ('declined', 'expired', 'failed') and card_id is not null and card_state is distinct from state)
       or (state in ('declined', 'failed') and said_at is null)
     ) order by updated_at limit $1`,
    [limit],
  );
  return r.rows.map((x) => x.payment_id);
}

/** A waiting payment whose approval ran out more than graceMs ago. The vault may still finish a charge at the edge. */
export async function expireOverdue(db: Db, now: Date, graceMs = 120_000): Promise<string[]> {
  const r = await db.query<{ payment_id: string }>(
    `update payments set state = 'expired', release_reason = 'expired', updated_at = $1
     where state = 'waiting' and approval_expires_at < $2 returning payment_id`,
    [now, new Date(now.getTime() - graceMs)],
  );
  return r.rows.map((x) => x.payment_id);
}

/** An ask that stopped halfway (the service restarted between the approval and the card) is given up. */
export async function interruptStale(db: Db, now: Date, ageMs = 60_000): Promise<string[]> {
  const r = await db.query<{ payment_id: string }>(
    `update payments set state = 'refused', refused_reason = 'interrupted', release_reason = 'other', updated_at = $1
     where state = 'asking' and created_at < $2 returning payment_id`,
    [now, new Date(now.getTime() - ageMs)],
  );
  return r.rows.map((x) => x.payment_id);
}

export async function paidApprovalIds(db: Db): Promise<string[]> {
  const r = await db.query<{ approval_id: string }>("select approval_id from payments where state = 'paid' and approval_id is not null");
  return r.rows.map((x) => x.approval_id);
}
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/money/ids.ts
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';

/**
 * A deterministic id for a write or a card made outside a composer turn, from what triggered it:
 * rid('go_paid', approvalId, 'booking') is "go_paid_<approval_id>_booking". The same trigger always gives the
 * same id, so a repeated callback or tap does each write once. Ids are at most 64 characters (the contract's Id),
 * so a long one becomes the prefix plus a hash of its parts.
 */
export function rid(prefix: string, ...parts: string[]): string {
  const plain = [prefix, ...parts].join('_');
  if (plain.length <= 64) return plain;
  return `${prefix}_${createHash('sha256').update(parts.join('|')).digest('hex')}`.slice(0, 64);
}

/** One payment row per spend request id: a rerun of the same ask finds the same row. */
export function paymentIdFor(tripId: string, spendRequestId: string): string {
  return `pay_${createHash('sha256').update(`${tripId}|${spendRequestId}`).digest('hex').slice(0, 16)}`;
}
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/money/signed.ts
import { signRequest, verifyRequest } from '@go/contract';
import type { FastifyInstance, FastifyRequest } from 'fastify';
import type pg from 'pg';

/** Go and the vault sign their calls to each other with VAULT_SHARED_SECRET, in the contract's signRequest format. */
export const VAULT_SIGNATURE_HEADER = 'x-vault-signature';

export function signVaultCall(secret: string, method: string, target: string, body: string, now: Date): string {
  return signRequest({ method, target, body }, secret, Math.floor(now.getTime() / 1000));
}

/** Checks the vault's signature over the exact body and accepts each signature once. */
export async function checkVaultCall(
  pool: pg.Pool,
  secret: string,
  req: { method: string; url: string; headers: Record<string, string | string[] | undefined> },
  body: string,
  now: Date,
): Promise<boolean> {
  const sent = req.headers[VAULT_SIGNATURE_HEADER];
  const header = Array.isArray(sent) ? sent[0] : sent;
  if (!verifyRequest({ method: req.method, target: req.url, body }, header, secret, Math.floor(now.getTime() / 1000)).ok) return false;
  const fields = new Map((header ?? '').split(',').map((p) => p.split('=') as [string, string]));
  const r = await pool.query('insert into vault_callback_seen (t, v1, seen_at) values ($1, $2, $3) on conflict do nothing', [
    Number(fields.get('t')),
    fields.get('v1'),
    now,
  ]);
  await pool.query('delete from vault_callback_seen where seen_at < $1', [new Date(now.getTime() - 600_000)]);
  return (r.rowCount ?? 0) > 0;
}

const raw = new WeakMap<FastifyRequest, string>();

export function rawBodyOf(req: FastifyRequest): string {
  return raw.get(req) ?? '';
}

/** Inside one Fastify scope: JSON bodies are parsed as usual and their exact text is kept for the signature check. */
export function keepRawJson(scope: FastifyInstance): void {
  if (scope.hasContentTypeParser('application/json')) scope.removeContentTypeParser('application/json');
  scope.addContentTypeParser('application/json', { parseAs: 'string' }, (req, body, done) => {
    const text = typeof body === 'string' ? body : body.toString('utf8');
    raw.set(req, text);
    try {
      done(null, text === '' ? {} : JSON.parse(text));
    } catch {
      done(Object.assign(new Error('The body is not JSON'), { statusCode: 400 }), undefined);
    }
  });
}
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/money/vault-client.ts
import { randomBytes } from 'node:crypto';
import { z } from 'zod';
import { signVaultCall, VAULT_SIGNATURE_HEADER } from './signed.js';

export type PaymentKind = 'fake' | 'stripe_test' | 'ixigo';
export type PayLinkRefusal =
  | 'bad_approval'
  | 'expired'
  | 'wrong_trip'
  | 'wrong_payer'
  | 'currency_mismatch'
  | 'over_amount'
  | 'hold_expiring'
  | 'rail_not_ready'
  | 'used'
  | 'unavailable';

const KNOWN: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set<PayLinkRefusal>([
  'bad_approval', 'expired', 'wrong_trip', 'wrong_payer', 'currency_mismatch', 'over_amount', 'hold_expiring', 'rail_not_ready', 'used',
]);

export interface PayLinkInput {
  approval: string;
  approvalId: string;
  tripId: string;
  payerPersonId: string;
  payerName: string;
  hold: { holdId: string; amountMinor: number; currency: string; merchant: string; paymentKind: PaymentKind; expiresAt: string };
}

export interface VaultClient {
  homeLink(i: { personId: string; start: 'home' | 'add_card' | 'add_login' }): Promise<{ url: string; expiresAt: string }>;
  payLink(i: PayLinkInput, o?: { timeoutMs?: number }): Promise<{ ok: true; url: string; expiresAt: string } | { ok: false; reason: PayLinkRefusal }>;
  bothKeys(): Promise<{ chargedApprovalIds: string[]; withoutBothKeys: number }>;
}

const LinkAnswer = z.object({ url: z.url({ protocol: /^https?$/ }), expires_at: z.iso.datetime({ offset: true }) });
const Refusal = z.object({ error: z.object({ reason: z.string() }) });
const BothKeys = z.object({ charged_approval_ids: z.array(z.string()), without_both_keys: z.int().nonnegative() });

export function vaultClient(o: { baseUrl: string; secret: string; fetch?: typeof fetch; now?: () => Date; timeoutMs?: number }): VaultClient {
  const send = o.fetch ?? fetch;
  const now = o.now ?? (() => new Date());
  const base = o.baseUrl.replace(/\/+$/, '');

  async function call(method: 'GET' | 'POST', path: string, body: Record<string, unknown> | null, timeoutMs: number): Promise<{ status: number; data: unknown }> {
    // A fresh nonce in every call keeps two identical calls in the same second from looking like a replay.
    const nonce = randomBytes(8).toString('hex');
    const target = method === 'GET' ? `${path}?n=${nonce}` : path;
    const text = body ? JSON.stringify({ ...body, nonce }) : '';
    const headers: Record<string, string> = { [VAULT_SIGNATURE_HEADER]: signVaultCall(o.secret, method, target, text, now()) };
    if (text) headers['content-type'] = 'application/json';
    const res = await send(`${base}${target}`, { method, headers, ...(text ? { body: text } : {}), signal: AbortSignal.timeout(timeoutMs) });
    let data: unknown = null;
    try {
      data = await res.json();
    } catch {
      data = null;
    }
    return { status: res.status, data };
  }

  return {
    async homeLink(i) {
      const r = await call('POST', '/internal/links', { person_id: i.personId, start: i.start }, o.timeoutMs ?? 1_500);
      if (r.status !== 200) throw new Error(`the vault answered ${r.status}`);
      const link = LinkAnswer.parse(r.data);
      return { url: link.url, expiresAt: link.expires_at };
    },
    async payLink(i, opts) {
      try {
        const r = await call(
          'POST',
          '/internal/pay-links',
          {
            approval: i.approval,
            approval_id: i.approvalId,
            trip_id: i.tripId,
            payer_person_id: i.payerPersonId,
            payer_name: i.payerName,
            hold: {
              hold_id: i.hold.holdId,
              amount_minor: i.hold.amountMinor,
              currency: i.hold.currency,
              merchant: i.hold.merchant,
              payment_kind: i.hold.paymentKind,
              expires_at: i.hold.expiresAt,
            },
          },
          opts?.timeoutMs ?? o.timeoutMs ?? 2_000,
        );
        if (r.status === 200) {
          const link = LinkAnswer.parse(r.data);
          return { ok: true, url: link.url, expiresAt: link.expires_at };
        }
        if (r.status === 409) {
          const refusal = Refusal.safeParse(r.data);
          const reason = refusal.success && KNOWN.has(refusal.data.error.reason) ? (refusal.data.error.reason as PayLinkRefusal) : 'unavailable';
          return { ok: false, reason };
        }
        return { ok: false, reason: 'unavailable' };
      } catch {
        return { ok: false, reason: 'unavailable' };
      }
    },
    async bothKeys() {
      const r = await call('GET', '/internal/both-keys', null, o.timeoutMs ?? 2_000);
      if (r.status !== 200) throw new Error(`the vault answered ${r.status}`);
      const b = BothKeys.parse(r.data);
      return { chargedApprovalIds: b.charged_approval_ids, withoutBothKeys: b.without_both_keys };
    },
  };
}
```

### Task 10: Go's words about money, and the pay, mandate and ticket cards

Every line Go says about money and every card it posts comes from one place, so the words stay short, warm and plain: rupees the Indian way, no em dashes, never a card detail. Each card is checked against the contract's own schema before it leaves.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/brain/src/money/words.ts`
- Create: `apps/brain/src/money/cards.ts`
- Test: `apps/brain/src/money/__tests__/words.test.ts`
- Test: `apps/brain/src/money/__tests__/cards.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `PayCard`, `MandateCard`, `TicketCard`, `BookingKind`, `Category`, `SpendDenialReason` from `@go/contract`.
- Produces:
  - `money(minor, currency) => string` ("₹54,200"); `shortDate(iso) => string` ("1 Nov"); `plain(text)` (em dashes out); `clip(text, max)`; `categoryFor(kind) => Category`; `type RefusalCode`; `interface Said { text: string; say: boolean }`; `refusalWords(code, { payer, what, amount?, cap? }) => Said`; `failReasonWords(reason) => string`; `bookedLine`, `failedLine`, `declinedLine`; `TOASTS`.
  - `type PayCard`, `type PayCardState`, `interface PayCardInput`, `payCard(input, state) => PayCard`; `mandateCard({ cardId, decisionId, what, capMinor, currency, refundableOnly, payerName }) => MandateCard`; `ticketCard({ cardId, bookingId, title, ref, refundableUntil }) => TicketCard`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

```ts
// apps/brain/src/money/__tests__/words.test.ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { bookedLine, categoryFor, clip, declinedLine, failedLine, money, plain, refusalWords, shortDate, TOASTS, type RefusalCode } from '../words.js';

const CODES: RefusalCode[] = [
  'decision_not_found', 'decision_not_closed', 'no_mandate', 'payer_declined', 'payer_has_not_confirmed', 'currency_mismatch',
  'over_cap', 'not_refundable', 'approval_outstanding', 'no_vote', 'wrong_offer', 'not_bookable', 'already_paid', 'no_travel', 'hold_failed',
  'rail_not_ready', 'vault_refused', 'server_down', 'closed',
];

describe('money words', () => {
  it('writes rupees the Indian way, paise only when there are any', () => {
    expect(money(5420000, 'INR')).toBe('₹54,200');
    expect(money(1084000, 'INR')).toBe('₹10,840');
    expect(money(5420050, 'INR')).toBe('₹54,200.50');
    expect(money(150000000, 'INR')).toBe('₹15,00,000');
  });

  it('writes a short date in India time', () => {
    expect(shortDate('2026-11-01T18:29:59Z')).toBe('1 Nov');
  });

  it('takes em dashes out and cuts long text', () => {
    expect(plain('Villa Sol \u2014 3 nights, 6\u20139 Nov')).toBe('Villa Sol, 3 nights, 6 to 9 Nov');
    expect(clip('a'.repeat(130), 120)).toHaveLength(120);
  });

  it('maps every booking kind to a spending category', () => {
    expect(categoryFor('stay')).toBe('stay');
    expect(categoryFor('flight')).toBe('getting_around');
    expect(categoryFor('cab')).toBe('getting_around');
    expect(categoryFor('table')).toBe('food');
    expect(categoryFor('activity')).toBe('activities');
    expect(categoryFor('other')).toBe('other');
  });

  it('has a plain line for every refusal, without em dashes or card details', () => {
    for (const code of CODES) {
      const said = refusalWords(code, { payer: 'Rahul', what: 'Villa Sol', amount: '₹56,400', cap: '₹55,000' });
      expect(said.text.length, code).toBeGreaterThan(10);
      expect(said.text, code).not.toMatch(/\u2014|card number|cvv/i);
    }
    expect(refusalWords('over_cap', { payer: 'Rahul', what: 'Villa Sol', amount: '₹56,400', cap: '₹55,000' }).text).toBe(
      'Villa Sol is now ₹56,400, above the ₹55,000 the group allowed. Raise the cap or pick another option?',
    );
    expect(refusalWords('decision_not_found', { payer: 'Rahul', what: 'it' }).say).toBe(false);
  });

  it('says what happened after a payment in one short line', () => {
    expect(bookedLine({ title: 'Villa Sol, 3 nights', payer: 'Rahul', amount: '₹54,200', people: 5 })).toBe(
      'Booked Villa Sol, 3 nights! Rahul paid ₹54,200, split between all 5.',
    );
    expect(failedLine({ payer: 'Rahul', what: 'Villa Sol', reason: 'card_declined' })).toBe(
      "Rahul, the payment for Villa Sol didn't go through (the bank declined the card). Nothing was charged. Want to try again?",
    );
    expect(declinedLine('Villa Sol')).toBe('Holding off on Villa Sol for now.');
    expect(TOASTS.notPayer('Rahul')).toBe('Only Rahul can approve this payment.');
  });
});
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/money/__tests__/cards.test.ts
import * as C from '@go/contract';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { mandateCard, payCard, ticketCard, type PayCardInput } from '../cards.js';

const villa: PayCardInput = {
  cardId: 'c_villa_pay',
  approvalId: 'ap_01JA7Q3V8Z',
  payerPersonId: 'p_rahul',
  payerName: 'Rahul',
  amountMinor: 5420000,
  currency: 'INR',
  merchant: 'ixigo: Villa Sol, Anjuna',
  purpose: 'Villa Sol, 3 nights, 6 to 9 Nov',
  refundableUntil: '2026-11-01T18:29:59Z',
  expiresAt: '2026-10-05T09:10:00Z',
};

describe('pay card', () => {
  it('waits for the payer, with buttons only the payer sees', () => {
    const card = payCard(villa, 'waiting');
    expect(C.Card.parse(card)).toEqual(card);
    expect(card).toMatchObject({
      kind: 'pay',
      state: 'waiting',
      for_person_ids: ['p_rahul'],
      approval_id: 'ap_01JA7Q3V8Z',
      amount_minor: 5420000,
      refundable_until: '2026-11-01T18:29:59Z',
      fallback_text: 'Rahul, approve ₹54,200 to ixigo: Villa Sol, Anjuna for Villa Sol, 3 nights, 6 to 9 Nov. Free cancellation until 1 Nov.',
    });
    expect(card.actions).toEqual([
      { action_id: 'approve', label: 'Approve', style: 'primary', only_for_person_ids: ['p_rahul'] },
      { action_id: 'decline', label: 'Decline', style: 'secondary', only_for_person_ids: ['p_rahul'] },
    ]);
  });

  it('drops the buttons and the pin once settled', () => {
    for (const state of ['paid', 'declined', 'failed'] as const) {
      const card = payCard(villa, state);
      expect(card.state).toBe(state);
      expect(card.actions ?? null).toBeNull();
      expect(card.for_person_ids ?? null).toBeNull();
    }
  });

  it('offers the payer a fresh start when it expired', () => {
    expect(payCard(villa, 'expired').actions).toEqual([
      { action_id: 'approve', label: 'Start again', style: 'primary', only_for_person_ids: ['p_rahul'] },
    ]);
  });

  it('never carries an em dash, even when the purpose had one', () => {
    const card = payCard({ ...villa, purpose: 'Villa Sol \u2014 3 nights' }, 'waiting');
    expect(JSON.stringify(card)).not.toMatch(/\u2014/);
  });

  it('leaves free cancellation out when there is none', () => {
    const card = payCard({ ...villa, refundableUntil: null }, 'waiting');
    expect(card.refundable_until ?? null).toBeNull();
    expect(card.fallback_text).not.toContain('Free cancellation');
  });
});

describe('mandate and ticket cards', () => {
  it('builds a mandate card for the vote, without pins', () => {
    const card = mandateCard({ cardId: 'c_m', decisionId: 'd_villa', what: 'Villa Sol', capMinor: 5500000, currency: 'INR', refundableOnly: true, payerName: 'Rahul' });
    expect(C.Card.parse(card)).toEqual(card);
    expect(card.fallback_text).toBe('Ready to book Villa Sol: up to ₹55,000, refundable only, Rahul pays. Rahul, open Go to say yes.');
    expect(card.for_person_ids ?? null).toBeNull();

  it('builds a ticket card for the booking', () => {
    const card = ticketCard({ cardId: 'c_t', bookingId: 'b_villa', title: 'Villa Sol, 3 nights', ref: 'IXG-7Q2K9', refundableUntil: '2026-11-01T18:29:59Z' });
    expect(C.Card.parse(card)).toEqual(card);
    expect(card.fallback_text).toBe('Booked: Villa Sol, 3 nights. Ref IXG-7Q2K9. Free cancellation until 1 Nov.');
  });
});
```
