```ts
// apps/brain/src/money/words.ts
import type * as C from '@go/contract';
import type { z } from 'zod';

type BookingKind = z.infer<typeof C.BookingKind>;
type Category = z.infer<typeof C.Category>;
type SpendDenialReason = z.infer<typeof C.SpendDenialReason>;

export function money(minor: number, currency: string): string {
  const whole = minor % 100 === 0;
  return new Intl.NumberFormat('en-IN', { style: 'currency', currency, minimumFractionDigits: whole ? 0 : 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 }).format(minor / 100);
}

/** "1 Nov", in India time, where these trips are planned. */
export function shortDate(iso: string): string {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB', { timeZone: 'Asia/Kolkata', day: 'numeric', month: 'short' }).format(new Date(iso));
}

/** No em dashes in anything a person sees: a dash between two numbers becomes "to" ("6 to 9"), a dash between words becomes a comma. */
export function plain(text: string): string {
  return text
    .replace(/(\d)\s*\u2013\s*(\d)/g, '$1 to $2')
    .replace(/\s*[\u2014\u2013]\s*/g, ', ')
    .trim();
}

export function clip(text: string, max: number): string {
  return text.length <= max ? text : `${text.slice(0, max - 1).trimEnd()}…`.slice(0, max);
}

export function categoryFor(kind: BookingKind): Category {
  switch (kind) {
    case 'stay':
      return 'stay';
    case 'flight':
    case 'train':
    case 'bus':
    case 'cab':
      return 'getting_around';
    case 'table':
      return 'food';
    case 'activity':
      return 'activities';
    case 'other':
      return 'other';
  }
}

export type RefusalCode =
  | SpendDenialReason
  | 'no_vote'
  | 'wrong_offer'
  /** The travel layer has no offer to book for the winner: a job found it by browsing, or Go never showed it. */
  | 'not_bookable'
  | 'already_paid'
  | 'no_travel'
  | 'hold_failed'
  | 'rail_not_ready'
  | 'vault_refused'
  | 'server_down'
  | 'closed';

export interface Said {
  text: string;
  /** False when the room should hear nothing (a bug on Go's side, logged instead). */
  say: boolean;
}

/** What Go says when it will not pay, in its own voice. The model may reword it; it may never offer another way to pay. */
export function refusalWords(code: RefusalCode, c: { payer: string; what: string; amount?: string; cap?: string }): Said {
  const yes = (text: string): Said => ({ text, say: true });
  switch (code) {
    case 'decision_not_found':
      return { text: "I couldn't find that vote.", say: false };
    case 'no_vote':
      return yes('I can only pay for something the group voted on, and the payer approves it on their own phone. Want me to put it to a vote?');
    case 'decision_not_closed':
      return yes("The vote is still open. I'll book the winner once it closes.");
    case 'no_mandate':
      return yes("The group hasn't set what to book yet: the choice, the cap and who pays.");
    case 'payer_declined':
      return yes(declinedLine(c.what));
    case 'payer_has_not_confirmed':
      return yes(`${c.payer}, tap Yes, book it on the card above when you're ready.`);
    case 'currency_mismatch':
      return yes(`${c.what} is priced in another currency now. Want me to check again?`);
    case 'over_cap':
      return yes(`${c.what} is now ${c.amount ?? 'more'}, above the ${c.cap ?? 'cap'} the group allowed. Raise the cap or pick another option?`);
    case 'not_refundable':
      return yes(`I can't find free cancellation on ${c.what} now. Want me to look for a refundable rate?`);
    case 'approval_outstanding':
      return yes(`${c.payer}, the payment for ${c.what} is already waiting on the card above.`);
    case 'wrong_offer':
      return yes(`That isn't what the group voted for. I can only pay for ${c.what}.`);
    case 'not_bookable':
      return yes("This one came from browsing; I can't book it directly.");
    case 'already_paid':
      return yes(`${c.what} is already booked.`);
    case 'no_travel':
      return yes("I can't book anything right now. Nothing was charged.");
    case 'hold_failed':
      return yes(`I couldn't hold ${c.what} just now. Want me to try again?`);
    case 'rail_not_ready':
      return yes("Paying this way isn't ready yet. Nothing was charged.");
    case 'vault_refused':
      return yes('The payment page refused this one. Nothing was charged. Want me to try again?');
    case 'server_down':
      return yes("I couldn't reach the trip just now. Nothing was charged. Ask me again in a moment.");
    case 'closed':
      return yes('That payment is already closed. Ask me again for a fresh one.');
  }
}

export function failReasonWords(reason: string | null): string {
  switch (reason) {
    case 'card_declined':
      return 'the bank declined the card';
    case 'needs_authentication':
      return 'the bank wanted an extra check';
    case 'rail_not_ready':
      return "that way of paying isn't ready yet";
    default:
      return 'the payment service had a problem';
  }
}

export function bookedLine(o: { title: string; payer: string; amount: string; people: number }): string {
  const split = o.people > 1 ? `, split between all ${o.people}` : '';
  return `Booked ${o.title}! ${o.payer} paid ${o.amount}${split}.`;
}

export function failedLine(o: { payer: string; what: string; reason: string | null }): string {
  return `${o.payer}, the payment for ${o.what} didn't go through (${failReasonWords(o.reason)}). Nothing was charged. Want to try again?`;
}

export function declinedLine(what: string): string {
  return `Holding off on ${what} for now.`;
}

export const TOASTS = {
  notPayer: (payer: string) => `Only ${payer} can approve this payment.`,
  notPay: "This card can't take payments.",
  gone: "This payment isn't open any more.",
  paid: 'Already paid.',
  declined: 'This payment was declined.',
  failed: "This payment didn't go through. Ask Go to try again.",
  notReady: "This payment isn't ready yet.",
  fresh: 'That one timed out. Setting up a fresh payment.',
  restart: 'Setting up a fresh payment.',
  noPage: "Couldn't open the payment page. Try again.",
  declinedDone: 'Declined. Nothing was charged.',
} as const;
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/money/cards.ts
import * as C from '@go/contract';
import type { z } from 'zod';
import { clip, money, plain, shortDate } from './words.js';

export type PayCard = z.infer<typeof C.PayCard>;
export type PayCardState = PayCard['state'];
export type MandateCard = z.infer<typeof C.MandateCard>;
export type TicketCard = z.infer<typeof C.TicketCard>;

export interface PayCardInput {
  cardId: string;
  approvalId: string;
  payerPersonId: string;
  payerName: string;
  amountMinor: number;
  currency: string;
  merchant: string;
  purpose: string;
  refundableUntil: string | null;
  expiresAt: string;
}

/** The pay card in each of its states. Only the payer sees the buttons; settled cards carry none. */
export function payCard(i: PayCardInput, state: PayCardState): PayCard {
  const amount = money(i.amountMinor, i.currency);
  const merchant = clip(plain(i.merchant), 120);
  const purpose = clip(plain(i.purpose), 200);
  const refund = i.refundableUntil ? ` Free cancellation until ${shortDate(i.refundableUntil)}.` : '';
  const fallback: Record<PayCardState, string> = {
    waiting: `${i.payerName}, approve ${amount} to ${merchant} for ${purpose}.${refund}`,
    paid: `Paid ${amount} to ${merchant} for ${purpose}.${refund}`,
    declined: `${i.payerName} declined ${amount} to ${merchant}.`,
    expired: `The payment of ${amount} to ${merchant} timed out.`,
    failed: `The payment of ${amount} to ${merchant} didn't go through. Nothing was charged.`,
  };
  const only = [i.payerPersonId];
  const actions =
    state === 'waiting'
      ? [
          { action_id: 'approve', label: 'Approve', style: 'primary' as const, only_for_person_ids: only },
          { action_id: 'decline', label: 'Decline', style: 'secondary' as const, only_for_person_ids: only },
        ]
      : state === 'expired'
        ? [{ action_id: 'approve', label: 'Start again', style: 'primary' as const, only_for_person_ids: only }]
        : null;
  return C.PayCard.parse({
    card_id: i.cardId,
    kind: 'pay',
    fallback_text: clip(plain(fallback[state]), 300),
    ...(state === 'waiting' ? { for_person_ids: only } : {}),
    approval_id: i.approvalId,
    payer_person_id: i.payerPersonId,
    amount_minor: i.amountMinor,
    currency: i.currency,
    merchant,
    purpose,
    ...(i.refundableUntil ? { refundable_until: i.refundableUntil } : {}),
    expires_at: i.expiresAt,
    state,
    ...(actions ? { actions } : {}),
  });
}

/** Posted right after set_mandate. The Server draws it and shows "Yes, book it" to the payer only, so Go pins nobody. */
export function mandateCard(o: {
  cardId: string;
  decisionId: string;
  what: string;
  capMinor: number;
  currency: string;
  refundableOnly: boolean;
  payerName: string;
}): MandateCard {
  const refundable = o.refundableOnly ? ', refundable only' : '';
  const text = `Ready to book ${o.what}: up to ${money(o.capMinor, o.currency)}${refundable}, ${o.payerName} pays. ${o.payerName}, open Go to say yes.`;
  return C.MandateCard.parse({ card_id: o.cardId, kind: 'mandate', decision_id: o.decisionId, fallback_text: clip(plain(text), 300) });
}

export function ticketCard(o: { cardId: string; bookingId: string; title: string; ref: string; refundableUntil: string | null }): TicketCard {
  const refund = o.refundableUntil ? ` Free cancellation until ${shortDate(o.refundableUntil)}.` : '';
  return C.TicketCard.parse({
    card_id: o.cardId,
    kind: 'ticket',
    booking_id: o.bookingId,
    fallback_text: clip(plain(`Booked: ${o.title}. Ref ${o.ref}.${refund}`), 300),
  });
}
```

---

### Task 11: Ask to pay, set the mandate, and the money test kit

`askToPay` is the only way a payment starts. It reads the trip from the Server (not from memory), refuses in plain words unless the vote is closed with a mandate the payer confirmed and the option asked for is the one the group chose (by the vote option's `option_ref`, which is an options card's `option_id`, never a travel offer id), turns that option into its travel offer with `brain.travel.offer(option_ref)` (and refuses "This one came from browsing; I can't book it directly." when there is no offer or its `offerId` is null), holds that offer with `brain.travel.hold(offerId, lead)`, checks the live price against the cap, asks the Server for its signed approval, asks the vault to check that approval and make a pay link, and only then hands over the pay card. Every write takes a stable id: from `ctx.stableId` inside a composer turn (the pay card then rides with Go's message in `ctx.cards`), or from its trigger outside a turn (an event id, a tap id). A rerun of the same ask finds its own `payments` row and carries on from where it stopped, so nothing is asked, approved or posted twice. `set_mandate` lets the composer record what the group allowed after a vote, under rules the code checks: the vote is closed, the choice is the winner, the payer is in the trip, and the cap is the price plus a small margin or a number someone said in the chat, never invented. The payer still has to tap "Yes, book it". The test kit plays the Server (with the guide's spend check and the contract's test signing key), the travel layer and the vault, so every money test runs offline against Postgres.

```ts
// apps/brain/src/money/__tests__/ask.test.ts
import * as C from '@go/contract';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { askToPay, idsFrom, idsFromTurn, withTurn } from '../ask.js';
import { getPayment } from '../store.js';
import { goaTrip, moneyKit, turnCtx, VILLA, type MoneyKit } from './kit.js';

let k: MoneyKit;
const payCards = () => [...k.server.cards.values()].filter((c) => c.kind === 'pay');
const spendAsks = () => k.server.writesOf('request_spend_approval');

beforeEach(async () => {
  k = await moneyKit();
});
afterEach(async () => {
  await k.drop();
});

describe('askToPay, when everything is in place', () => {
  it('turns the winning option into its offer, holds that offer, gets the Server approval, has the vault check it, then posts the pay card for the payer', async () => {
    const r = await k.ask();
    if (!('cardId' in r)) throw new Error(r.refused);
    // The vote names Villa Sol by its option_ref; the hold goes to the offer the travel layer gives for it.
    expect(k.travel.looked).toEqual(['opt_villa_sol']);
    expect(k.travel.holds).toEqual([
      { offerId: 'ixigo:stay:villa-sol-anjuna', lead: { name: 'Rahul', personIds: ['p_rahul', 'p_priya', 'p_arjun', 'p_meher', 'p_kabir'] }, holdId: 'hold_1' },
    ]);
    expect(spendAsks()[0]?.params).toMatchObject({
      request_id: 'go_spend_d_villa_trigger_1',
      decision_id: 'd_villa',
      amount_minor: 5420000,
      currency: 'INR',
      merchant: 'ixigo: Villa Sol, Anjuna',
      purpose: 'Villa Sol, 3 nights, 6 to 9 Nov',
      refundable_until: '2026-11-01T18:29:59Z',
    });
    const approvalId = [...k.server.approvals.keys()][0];
    expect(k.vault.payLinks[0]).toMatchObject({ approvalId, tripId: 't_goa', payerPersonId: 'p_rahul', hold: { holdId: 'hold_1', amountMinor: 5420000, paymentKind: 'fake' } });
    expect(r.cardId).toBe('c_pay_d_villa_trigger_1');
    expect(C.PayCard.parse(k.server.cards.get(r.cardId))).toMatchObject({ state: 'waiting', payer_person_id: 'p_rahul', for_person_ids: ['p_rahul'], approval_id: approvalId });
    expect(k.server.writesOf('post_message')[0]?.params.request_id).toBe('go_paycard_d_villa_trigger_1');
    expect(await getPayment(k.pool, r.paymentId)).toMatchObject({ state: 'waiting', card_id: r.cardId, card_state: 'waiting', approval_id: approvalId, label: 'Villa Sol' });
  });

  it('in a composer turn, takes its ids from the turn and lets the card ride with Go\'s message', async () => {
    const ctx = turnCtx('t_goa', k.server, k.server.trip('t_goa'));
    const r = await askToPay(k.deps, 't_goa', VILLA, idsFromTurn(ctx), withTurn(ctx));
    if (!('cardId' in r)) throw new Error(r.refused);
    expect(spendAsks()[0]?.params.request_id).toBe('go_spend_turn_test_1');
    expect(ctx.cards).toEqual([expect.objectContaining({ kind: 'pay', card_id: 'c_pay_turn_test_1' })]);
    expect(payCards()).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('does nothing twice when the same ask runs again, and carries on from where it stopped', async () => {
    const first = await k.ask(VILLA, 'trigger_x');
    if (!('cardId' in first)) throw new Error(first.refused);
    expect(await k.ask(VILLA, 'trigger_x')).toEqual(first);
    await k.pool.query("update payments set state = 'asking', card_state = null");
    expect(await k.ask(VILLA, 'trigger_x')).toEqual(first);
    expect(spendAsks()).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(k.server.writesOf('post_message')).toHaveLength(1);
    expect((await getPayment(k.pool, first.paymentId))?.state).toBe('waiting');
  });

  it('names the voted option in the purpose even when the model left it out', async () => {
    await k.ask({ ...VILLA, purpose: '3 nights in Anjuna' });
    expect(spendAsks()[0]?.params.purpose).toBe('Villa Sol: 3 nights in Anjuna');
  });
});

describe('askToPay refuses, in plain words, and posts nothing', () => {
  const expectNothingPosted = () => {
    expect(payCards()).toEqual([]);
    expect(spendAsks()).toEqual([]);
  };

  it('without a vote', async () => {
    const r = await k.ask({ decisionId: 'd_scuba', offerId: 'opt_scuba', purpose: 'Scuba' });
    expect(r).toMatchObject({ code: 'no_vote', refused: expect.stringContaining('voted on') });
    expectNothingPosted();
    expect(k.travel.holds).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('while the vote is open', async () => {
    k.server.trips.set('t_goa', goaTrip('open'));
    expect(await k.ask()).toMatchObject({ code: 'decision_not_closed' });
    expectNothingPosted();
  });

  it('without a mandate', async () => {
    k.server.trips.set('t_goa', goaTrip('closed'));
    expect(await k.ask()).toMatchObject({ code: 'no_mandate' });
    expectNothingPosted();
  });

  it('before the payer taps Yes, book it', async () => {
    k.server.trips.set('t_goa', goaTrip('mandate'));
    expect(await k.ask()).toMatchObject({ code: 'payer_has_not_confirmed', refused: "Rahul, tap Yes, book it on the card above when you're ready." });
    expectNothingPosted();
  });

  it('after the payer said not now', async () => {
    k.server.trips.set('t_goa', goaTrip('declined'));
    expect(await k.ask()).toMatchObject({ code: 'payer_declined', refused: 'Holding off on Villa Sol for now.' });
    expectNothingPosted();
  });

  it('for an offer the group did not choose', async () => {
    expect(await k.ask({ ...VILLA, offerId: 'opt_casa_azul' })).toMatchObject({ code: 'wrong_offer' });
    // A travel offer id is not how the vote names its options either.
    expect(await k.ask({ ...VILLA, offerId: 'ixigo:stay:villa-sol-anjuna' })).toMatchObject({ code: 'wrong_offer' });
    expectNothingPosted();
    expect(k.travel.holds).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('when the winner came from browsing, or the travel layer has no offer for it, and holds nothing', async () => {
    k.travel.options.set('opt_villa_sol', null);
    expect(await k.ask()).toMatchObject({ code: 'not_bookable', refused: "This one came from browsing; I can't book it directly." });
    k.travel.options.delete('opt_villa_sol');
    expect(await k.ask()).toMatchObject({ code: 'not_bookable' });
    expectNothingPosted();
    expect(k.travel.holds).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('when the live price is above the cap, and says the new price', async () => {
    k.travel.price('ixigo:stay:villa-sol-anjuna', 5640000);
    expect(await k.ask()).toMatchObject({
      code: 'over_cap',
      refused: 'Villa Sol is now ₹56,400, above the ₹55,000 the group allowed. Raise the cap or pick another option?',
    });
    expectNothingPosted();
  });

  it('when free cancellation is gone and the group asked for refundable only', async () => {
    k.travel.offers.set('ixigo:stay:villa-sol-anjuna', { ...k.travel.priced('ixigo:stay:villa-sol-anjuna'), refundableUntil: null });
    expect(await k.ask()).toMatchObject({ code: 'not_refundable' });
    expectNothingPosted();
  });

  it('when the price is in another currency', async () => {
    k.travel.offers.set('ixigo:stay:villa-sol-anjuna', { ...k.travel.priced('ixigo:stay:villa-sol-anjuna'), currency: 'USD' });
    expect(await k.ask()).toMatchObject({ code: 'currency_mismatch' });
    expectNothingPosted();
  });

  it('when the hold fails or there is no travel layer', async () => {
    k.travel.failHold = true;
    expect(await k.ask()).toMatchObject({ code: 'hold_failed' });
    const ids = idsFrom('no_travel', 'd_villa');
    expect(await askToPay({ ...k.deps, travel: () => undefined }, 't_goa', VILLA, ids, async () => undefined)).toMatchObject({ code: 'no_travel' });
    expectNothingPosted();
  });

  it('a second time while a pay card is waiting', async () => {
    await k.ask(VILLA, 'first');
    expect(await k.ask(VILLA, 'second')).toMatchObject({ code: 'approval_outstanding' });
    expect(payCards()).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('when the Server denies, and posts no card', async () => {
    const first = await k.ask(VILLA, 'first');
    if (!('cardId' in first)) throw new Error('first ask refused');
    await k.pool.query('delete from payments');
    expect(await k.ask(VILLA, 'second')).toMatchObject({ code: 'approval_outstanding' });
    expect(payCards()).toHaveLength(1);
    const rows = await k.pool.query('select state, refused_reason from payments');
    expect(rows.rows).toEqual([{ state: 'refused', refused_reason: 'approval_outstanding' }]);
  });

  it('when the vault refuses the approval, and keeps the approval for release', async () => {
    k.vault.refuse = 'over_amount';
    expect(await k.ask()).toMatchObject({ code: 'vault_refused' });
    expect(payCards()).toEqual([]);
    const row = (await k.pool.query('select state, refused_reason, release_reason, approval_id from payments')).rows[0];
    expect(row).toMatchObject({ state: 'refused', refused_reason: 'vault_over_amount', release_reason: 'other' });
    expect(row.approval_id).toMatch(/^ap_/);
  });

  it('once the vote is already paid for', async () => {
    const first = await k.ask(VILLA, 'first');
    if (!('cardId' in first)) throw new Error('first ask refused');
    await k.pool.query("update payments set state = 'paid'");
    expect(await k.ask(VILLA, 'second')).toMatchObject({ code: 'already_paid', refused: 'Villa Sol is already booked.' });
  });
});
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/money/__tests__/mandate.test.ts
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { askToPay, idsFromTurn, withTurn } from '../ask.js';
import { amountsSaid, marginCap, setMandate } from '../mandate.js';
import { AskToPayInput, SetMandateInput, askToPayTool, setMandateTool } from '../tools.js';
import { goaTrip, moneyKit, runToolCalls, say, turnCtx, type MoneyKit } from './kit.js';

let k: MoneyKit;
beforeEach(async () => {
  k = await moneyKit({ trip: goaTrip('closed') });
});
afterEach(async () => {
  await k.drop();
});

const mandate = { decisionId: 'd_villa', choiceIndex: 0, capMinor: 5500000, refundableOnly: true, payerPersonId: 'p_rahul' };
const ctxWith = (lines: Array<[string, string]> = []) =>
  turnCtx('t_goa', k.server, k.server.trip('t_goa'), lines.map(([who, text], i) => say(i + 1, who, text)));

describe('amounts people said', () => {
  it('reads rupees, k, lakh and per head', () => {
    const said = amountsSaid([say(1, 'p_rahul', 'max 60k for the villa'), say(2, 'p_priya', 'ok under ₹1,20,000 total'), say(3, 'p_arjun', '12k a head is fine')], 5);
    expect(said).toContain(6000000); // 60k
    expect(said).toContain(12000000); // 1,20,000
    expect(said).toContain(1200000 * 5); // 12k a head, five people
    expect(amountsSaid([say(1, 'p_rahul', 'Goa on 6 to 9 Nov')], 5)).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe('set_mandate', () => {
  it('records what the group allowed and puts the mandate card in the turn', async () => {
    const ctx = ctxWith();
    const r = await setMandate(k.deps, ctx, mandate);
    if (!('cardId' in r)) throw new Error(r.refused);
    expect(k.server.writesOf('set_mandate')[0]?.params).toMatchObject({
      request_id: 'go_mandate_turn_test_1',
      decision_id: 'd_villa',
      choice_index: 0,
      cap_minor: 5500000,
      currency: 'INR',
      refundable_only: true,
      payer_person_id: 'p_rahul',
    });
    expect(ctx.cards).toEqual([expect.objectContaining({ kind: 'mandate', card_id: 'c_mandate_turn_test_1', decision_id: 'd_villa' })]);
  });

  it('allows the price plus a small margin, and no more unless someone said it', async () => {
    expect(marginCap(5420000)).toBe(6000000);
    expect(await setMandate(k.deps, ctxWith(), { ...mandate, capMinor: 6000000 })).toMatchObject({ cardId: expect.any(String) });
    k.server.trips.set('t_goa', goaTrip('closed'));
    expect(await setMandate(k.deps, ctxWith(), { ...mandate, capMinor: 9000000 })).toMatchObject({ refused: expect.stringContaining('₹60,000') });
    expect(await setMandate(k.deps, ctxWith([['p_rahul', 'we can go up to 90k for a good villa']]), { ...mandate, capMinor: 9000000 })).toMatchObject({
      cardId: expect.any(String),
    });
  });

  it('refuses a cap below the price', async () => {
    expect(await setMandate(k.deps, ctxWith(), { ...mandate, capMinor: 5000000 })).toMatchObject({ refused: expect.stringContaining('below the price') });
    expect(k.server.writesOf('set_mandate')).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('refuses an option that did not win', async () => {
    expect(await setMandate(k.deps, ctxWith(), { ...mandate, choiceIndex: 1 })).toMatchObject({ refused: 'The group picked Villa Sol. I can only set that.' });
  });

  it('refuses a payer who is not in the trip, and an open vote', async () => {
    expect(await setMandate(k.deps, ctxWith(), { ...mandate, payerPersonId: 'p_stranger' })).toMatchObject({ refused: "That person isn't in this trip." });
    k.server.trips.set('t_goa', goaTrip('open'));
    expect(await setMandate(k.deps, ctxWith(), mandate)).toMatchObject({ refused: expect.stringContaining('still open') });
    expect(k.server.writesOf('set_mandate')).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("does not set the same mandate twice, which would clear the payer's yes", async () => {
    k.server.trips.set('t_goa', goaTrip('confirmed'));
    expect(await setMandate(k.deps, ctxWith(), mandate)).toMatchObject({ refused: expect.stringContaining('already set') });
    expect(k.server.writesOf('set_mandate')).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe('the quick tools', () => {
  const tools = () => [
    askToPayTool((ctx, o) => askToPay(k.deps, ctx.tripId, o, idsFromTurn(ctx), withTurn(ctx))),
    setMandateTool((ctx, o) => setMandate(k.deps, ctx, o)),
  ];

  it('ask_to_pay takes only the vote, the offer and the purpose: no payer, no card', () => {
    const base = { decision_id: 'd_villa', option_ref: 'opt_villa_sol', purpose: 'Villa Sol' };
    expect(AskToPayInput.safeParse(base).success).toBe(true);
    expect(AskToPayInput.safeParse({ ...base, payer_person_id: 'p_priya' }).success).toBe(false);
    expect(AskToPayInput.safeParse({ ...base, card_number: '4242424242424242' }).success).toBe(false);
    expect(SetMandateInput.safeParse({ decision_id: 'd_villa', choice_index: 0, cap_minor: 1, refundable_only: true, payer_person_id: 'p_rahul', card: 'x' }).success).toBe(false);
  });

  it("ask_to_pay pays only in the turn's own trip and hands a refusal back for the model to say", async () => {
    const ctx = ctxWith();
    const [out] = await runToolCalls(tools(), ctx, [{ name: 'ask_to_pay', input: { decision_id: 'd_villa', option_ref: 'opt_villa_sol', purpose: 'Villa Sol' } }]);
    expect(out).toMatchObject({ refused: expect.stringContaining("hasn't set what to book"), tell_the_room: expect.stringContaining('Never offer another way to pay') });
  });

  it('set_mandate runs from the model with the turn ids', async () => {
    const ctx = ctxWith();
    const [out] = await runToolCalls(tools(), ctx, [
      { name: 'set_mandate', input: { decision_id: 'd_villa', choice_index: 0, cap_minor: 5500000, refundable_only: true, payer_person_id: 'p_rahul' } },
    ]);
    expect(out).toMatchObject({ mandate_card: 'c_mandate_turn_test_1' });
  });
});
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/money/deps.ts
import type { FastifyBaseLogger } from 'fastify';
import type pg from 'pg';
import type { ServerLink } from '../door/server-link.js';
import type { Travel } from '../travel/travel.js';
import type { PaymentKind, VaultClient } from './vault-client.js';
import { clip, plain } from './words.js';

export interface MoneyDeps {
  pool: pg.Pool;
  link: ServerLink;
  /** The travel layer, read on every use (P4 sets brain.travel). */
  travel: () => Travel | undefined;
  vault: VaultClient;
  /** PAY_RAIL, read on every ask so the demo switch flips without a redeploy. */
  payRail: () => PaymentKind;
  now: () => Date;
  log: FastifyBaseLogger;
  /** The vault call inside an Approve tap. P1's tap router answers within 2.5 s. */
  tapVaultMs?: number;
}

/** One line in Go's voice, outside a turn. The request id comes from what triggered it, so a retry posts it once. */
export async function postLine(d: Pick<MoneyDeps, 'link'>, tripId: string, text: string, requestId: string): Promise<void> {
  await d.link.request('post_message', { request_id: requestId, trip_id: tripId, text: clip(plain(text), 2000) });
}

/** Work done after an answer was sent. Errors are logged, and tests can wait for all of it. */
export class Later {
  private readonly running = new Set<Promise<void>>();

  run(log: FastifyBaseLogger, label: string, fn: () => Promise<unknown>): void {
    const p: Promise<void> = fn()
      .then(() => undefined)
      .catch((e: unknown) => {
        log.error({ err: e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'unknown' }, `${label} failed`);
      })
      .finally(() => {
        this.running.delete(p);
      });
    this.running.add(p);
  }

  async settled(): Promise<void> {
    while (this.running.size > 0) await Promise.allSettled([...this.running]);
  }
}
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/money/ask.ts
import type { TurnCtx } from '../composer/tools.js';
import type { ToServerResult, Trip } from '../door/methods.js';
import type { Hold, Travel } from '../travel/travel.js';
import { payCard, type PayCard, type PayCardState } from './cards.js';
import type { MoneyDeps } from './deps.js';
import { paymentIdFor, rid } from './ids.js';
import { getPayment, insertAsking, openForDecision, paidForDecision, patchPayment, transition, type PaymentRow } from './store.js';
import type { PaymentKind } from './vault-client.js';
import { clip, money, plain, refusalWords, type RefusalCode } from './words.js';

export type AskResult = { cardId: string; paymentId: string } | { refused: string; code: RefusalCode; say: boolean };

/** The ids one ask writes with. The spend request id also names the payments row. */
export interface AskIds {
  spend: string;
  card: string;
  cardId: string;
}

/** How the pay card reaches the room: with Go's message in a turn, or posted by money itself outside one. */
export type Deliver = (card: PayCard) => Promise<void>;

/** In a composer turn: a rerun of a crashed turn repeats these ids. */
export function idsFromTurn(ctx: Pick<TurnCtx, 'stableId'>): AskIds {
  return { spend: ctx.stableId('go_spend'), card: ctx.stableId('go_paycard'), cardId: ctx.stableId('c_pay') };
}

/** Outside a turn: from the event or tap that started the ask, e.g. go_spend_<decision_id>_<event_id>. */
export function idsFrom(trigger: string, decisionId: string): AskIds {
  return { spend: rid('go_spend', decisionId, trigger), card: rid('go_paycard', decisionId, trigger), cardId: rid('c_pay', decisionId, trigger) };
}

export function withTurn(ctx: Pick<TurnCtx, 'cards'>): Deliver {
  return async (card) => {
    ctx.cards.push(card);
  };
}

export function postCard(d: Pick<MoneyDeps, 'link'>, tripId: string, requestId: string): Deliver {
  return async (card) => {
    await d.link.request('post_message', { request_id: requestId, trip_id: tripId, card });
  };
}

/** An ask started by an event or a tap: ids from its trigger, and the card posted right away. */
export function askOutsideTurn(d: MoneyDeps, tripId: string, o: { decisionId: string; offerId: string; purpose: string }, trigger: string): Promise<AskResult> {
  const ids = idsFrom(trigger, o.decisionId);
  return askToPay(d, tripId, o, ids, postCard(d, tripId, ids.card));
}

interface Words {
  payer: string;
  what: string;
  amount?: string;
  cap?: string;
}

function refuse(d: MoneyDeps, code: RefusalCode, words: Words, err?: unknown): AskResult {
  const said = refusalWords(code, words);
  d.log.info({ code, ...(err instanceof Error ? { err: err.message } : {}) }, 'ask to pay refused');
  return { refused: said.text, code, say: said.say };
}

const CODES: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set<RefusalCode>([
  'decision_not_found', 'decision_not_closed', 'no_mandate', 'payer_has_not_confirmed', 'payer_declined', 'over_cap',
  'currency_mismatch', 'not_refundable', 'approval_outstanding', 'no_vote', 'wrong_offer', 'not_bookable', 'already_paid', 'no_travel',
  'hold_failed', 'rail_not_ready', 'vault_refused', 'server_down', 'closed',
]);

function codeOf(reason: string | null): RefusalCode {
  if (reason === 'vault_rail_not_ready') return 'rail_not_ready';
  if (reason?.startsWith('vault_')) return 'vault_refused';
  return reason && CODES.has(reason) ? (reason as RefusalCode) : 'server_down';
}

/**
 * Free cancellation, exactly as the travel layer gave it on the hold (it is signed into the approval). Without it a
 * refundable-only mandate is refused, never guessed.
 */
function refundableUntilOf(hold: Hold): string | null {
  return hold.refundableUntil ?? null;
}

/** The payer always sees the name of what the group voted for. */
function purposeFor(label: string, asked: string): string {
  const text = plain(asked);
  return clip(text.toLowerCase().includes(label.toLowerCase()) ? text : `${label}: ${text}`, 200);
}

/** The pay card for a payments row in a given state, or null when the row has no card yet. */
export function payCardOf(row: PaymentRow, state: PayCardState): PayCard | null {
  if (!row.card_id || !row.approval_id || !row.approval_expires_at || row.amount_minor === null || !row.currency || !row.merchant) return null;
  return payCard(
    {
      cardId: row.card_id,
      approvalId: row.approval_id,
      payerPersonId: row.payer_person_id,
      payerName: row.payer_name,
      amountMinor: row.amount_minor,
      currency: row.currency,
      merchant: row.merchant,
      purpose: row.purpose,
      refundableUntil: row.refundable_until,
      expiresAt: row.approval_expires_at.toISOString(),
    },
    state,
  );
}

/**
 * `o.offerId` keeps master §6.3's name for Pay.askToPay, but it carries the winning vote option's `option_ref` (an options
 * card's option_id). It is checked against the vote, then turned into the travel offer with `travel.offer`; it is never
 * held as it is.
 */
export async function askToPay(
  d: MoneyDeps,
  tripId: string,
  o: { decisionId: string; offerId: string; purpose: string },
  ids: AskIds,
  deliver: Deliver,
): Promise<AskResult> {
  const optionRef = o.offerId;
  const paymentId = paymentIdFor(tripId, ids.spend);
  const known = await getPayment(d.pool, paymentId);
  if (known) return advance(d, known, ids, deliver);

  const now = d.now();
  let trip: Trip;
  try {
    trip = await d.link.request('read_trip', { trip_id: tripId });
  } catch (e) {
    return refuse(d, 'server_down', { payer: 'The payer', what: 'it' }, e);
  }
  const nameOf = (id: string) => trip.people.find((p) => p.person_id === id)?.name ?? 'The payer';
  const decision = trip.decisions.find((x) => x.decision_id === o.decisionId);
  if (!decision || decision.kind !== 'choice') return refuse(d, 'no_vote', { payer: 'The payer', what: 'it' });
  const m = decision.mandate ?? null;
  const chosen = decision.options[m?.choice_index ?? decision.result_index ?? 0];
  const words: Words = { payer: m ? nameOf(m.payer_person_id) : 'The payer', what: chosen?.label ?? 'it' };

  // What the group decided, read from the Server's rows.
  if (decision.status !== 'closed') return refuse(d, 'decision_not_closed', words);
  if (!m) return refuse(d, 'no_mandate', words);
  if (m.declined_at && (!m.confirmed_at || Date.parse(m.declined_at) > Date.parse(m.confirmed_at))) return refuse(d, 'payer_declined', words);
  if (!m.confirmed_at) return refuse(d, 'payer_has_not_confirmed', words);
  if (!chosen?.option_ref || chosen.option_ref !== optionRef) return refuse(d, 'wrong_offer', words);
  if (await paidForDecision(d.pool, tripId, o.decisionId)) return refuse(d, 'already_paid', words);
  if (await openForDecision(d.pool, tripId, o.decisionId)) return refuse(d, 'approval_outstanding', words);

  const travel = d.travel();
  if (!travel) return refuse(d, 'no_travel', words);
  // The vote names the winner by its option_ref; the travel layer says which offer that is. An option a job found by
  // browsing has no offer id, so Go cannot book it.
  let offer: Awaited<ReturnType<Travel['offer']>>;
  try {
    offer = await travel.offer(optionRef);
  } catch (e) {
    return refuse(d, 'hold_failed', words, e);
  }
  const offerId = offer?.offerId ?? null;
  if (!offerId) return refuse(d, 'not_bookable', words);

  // The live price, checked now.
  const people = trip.people.map((p) => p.person_id);
  let hold: Hold;
  try {
    hold = await travel.hold(offerId, { name: words.payer, personIds: people });
  } catch (e) {
    return refuse(d, 'hold_failed', words, e);
  }
  if (hold.currency !== m.currency) return refuse(d, 'currency_mismatch', words);
  if (hold.amountMinor > m.cap_minor) {
    return refuse(d, 'over_cap', { ...words, amount: money(hold.amountMinor, hold.currency), cap: money(m.cap_minor, m.currency) });
  }
  const refundableUntil = refundableUntilOf(hold);
  if (m.refundable_only && !(refundableUntil && Date.parse(refundableUntil) > now.getTime())) return refuse(d, 'not_refundable', words);
  if (Date.parse(hold.expiresAt) <= now.getTime() + 60_000) return refuse(d, 'hold_failed', words);

  const kind: PaymentKind = d.payRail();
  const inserted = await insertAsking(
    d.pool,
    {
      payment_id: paymentId,
      trip_id: tripId,
      decision_id: o.decisionId,
      option_ref: optionRef,
      purpose: purposeFor(words.what, o.purpose),
      label: words.what,
      payer_person_id: m.payer_person_id,
      payer_name: words.payer,
      for_person_ids: people,
      hold_id: hold.holdId,
      amount_minor: hold.amountMinor,
      currency: hold.currency,
      merchant: clip(plain(hold.merchant), 120),
      payment_kind: kind,
      hold_expires_at: new Date(hold.expiresAt),
      refundable_until: refundableUntil,
    },
    now,
  );
  // A rerun that raced its own first run finds that row; anything else is a second ask for a vote already waiting.
  const row = await getPayment(d.pool, paymentId);
  if (!row) return refuse(d, inserted === 'busy' ? 'approval_outstanding' : 'server_down', words);
  return advance(d, row, ids, deliver);
}

/** Takes a payments row as far as it can go: the Server's approval, the vault's link, the pay card. Safe to repeat. */
async function advance(d: MoneyDeps, row: PaymentRow, ids: AskIds, deliver: Deliver): Promise<AskResult> {
  const words: Words = { payer: row.payer_name, what: row.label };
  switch (row.state) {
    case 'paid':
      return refuse(d, 'already_paid', words);
    case 'declined':
      return refuse(d, 'payer_declined', words);
    case 'refused':
      return refuse(d, codeOf(row.refused_reason), words);
    case 'expired':
    case 'failed':
      return refuse(d, 'closed', words);
    case 'waiting': {
      const card = payCardOf(row, 'waiting');
      if (!card || !row.card_id) return refuse(d, 'closed', words);
      await deliver(card);
      return { cardId: row.card_id, paymentId: row.payment_id };
    }
    case 'asking':
      break;
  }
  if (row.amount_minor === null || !row.currency || !row.merchant || !row.hold_id || !row.hold_expires_at) return refuse(d, 'server_down', words);
  const amountMinor = row.amount_minor;
  const fail = async (reason: string, code: RefusalCode, err?: unknown): Promise<AskResult> => {
    await transition(d.pool, row.payment_id, ['asking'], 'refused', { refused_reason: reason, release_reason: 'other' }, d.now());
    return refuse(d, code, words, err);
  };

  // 1. The Server's key: its spend check reads only its own rows and signs a one-use approval.
  let approvalId = row.approval_id;
  let token = row.approval_token;
  let expiresAt = row.approval_expires_at;
  if (!approvalId || !token || !expiresAt) {
    let answer: ToServerResult<'request_spend_approval'>;
    try {
      answer = await d.link.request('request_spend_approval', {
        request_id: ids.spend,
        trip_id: row.trip_id,
        decision_id: row.decision_id,
        amount_minor: amountMinor,
        currency: row.currency,
        merchant: row.merchant,
        purpose: row.purpose,
        ...(row.refundable_until ? { refundable_until: row.refundable_until } : {}),
      });
    } catch (e) {
      return fail('server_down', 'server_down', e);
    }
    if (answer.status === 'denied') {
      await transition(d.pool, row.payment_id, ['asking'], 'refused', { refused_reason: answer.reason }, d.now());
      return refuse(d, answer.reason, words);
    }
    approvalId = answer.approval_id;
    token = answer.approval;
    expiresAt = new Date(answer.expires_at);
    await patchPayment(d.pool, row.payment_id, { approval_id: approvalId, approval_token: token, approval_expires_at: expiresAt }, d.now());
  }

  // 2. The vault checks the approval before anything is shown, so no pay card appears for a payment it would refuse.
  const link = await d.vault.payLink({
    approval: token,
    approvalId,
    tripId: row.trip_id,
    payerPersonId: row.payer_person_id,
    payerName: row.payer_name,
    hold: {
      holdId: row.hold_id,
      amountMinor,
      currency: row.currency,
      merchant: row.merchant,
      paymentKind: (row.payment_kind ?? 'fake') as PaymentKind,
      expiresAt: row.hold_expires_at.toISOString(),
    },
  });
  if (!link.ok) return fail(`vault_${link.reason}`, link.reason === 'rail_not_ready' ? 'rail_not_ready' : 'vault_refused');

  // 3. The pay card, for the payer only.
  const cardId = row.card_id ?? ids.cardId;
  await patchPayment(d.pool, row.payment_id, { card_id: cardId, pay_link_url: link.url, pay_link_expires_at: new Date(link.expiresAt) }, d.now());
  const card = payCard(
    {
      cardId,
      approvalId,
      payerPersonId: row.payer_person_id,
      payerName: row.payer_name,
      amountMinor,
      currency: row.currency,
      merchant: row.merchant,
      purpose: row.purpose,
      refundableUntil: row.refundable_until,
      expiresAt: expiresAt.toISOString(),
    },
    'waiting',
  );
  try {
    await deliver(card);
  } catch (e) {
    return fail('server_down', 'server_down', e);
  }
  await transition(d.pool, row.payment_id, ['asking'], 'waiting', { card_state: 'waiting' }, d.now());
  return { cardId, paymentId: row.payment_id };
}
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/money/mandate.ts
import type { TurnCtx } from '../composer/tools.js';
import type { Message, Trip } from '../door/methods.js';
import { mandateCard } from './cards.js';
import type { MoneyDeps } from './deps.js';
import { money, refusalWords } from './words.js';

/** The price plus 10%, rounded up to the next ₹1,000: the most Go sets on its own. */
export function marginCap(priceMinor: number): number {
  return Math.ceil((priceMinor * 1.1) / 100_000) * 100_000;
}

const UNITS: Record<string, number> = { k: 1_000, thousand: 1_000, l: 100_000, lakh: 100_000, lac: 100_000 };

/** Every amount people wrote in the chat, in paise, also times the number of people (for "12k a head"). */
export function amountsSaid(messages: Message[], people: number): number[] {
  const out = new Set<number>();
  for (const m of messages.slice(-200)) {
    if (m.author.kind !== 'person' || !m.text) continue;
    for (const hit of m.text.matchAll(/(?:₹|rs\.?|inr)?\s*(\d{1,3}(?:,\d{2,3})+|\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*(k|thousand|lakh|lac|l)?\b/gi)) {
      const rupees = Number((hit[1] ?? '').replace(/,/g, '')) * (UNITS[(hit[2] ?? '').toLowerCase()] ?? 1);
      if (!Number.isFinite(rupees) || rupees < 100) continue;
      out.add(Math.round(rupees * 100));
      out.add(Math.round(rupees * 100 * people));
    }
  }
  return [...out];
}

/**
 * Records what the group allowed after a closed choice vote, and puts the mandate card in the turn.
 * It cannot pay anything: the payer still taps "Yes, book it", and paying still needs both keys.
 */
export async function setMandate(
  d: MoneyDeps,
  ctx: Pick<TurnCtx, 'tripId' | 'stableId' | 'cards' | 'pack'>,
  o: { decisionId: string; choiceIndex: number; capMinor: number; refundableOnly: boolean; payerPersonId: string },
): Promise<{ cardId: string } | { refused: string }> {
  const vague = { payer: 'The payer', what: 'it' };
  let trip: Trip;
  try {
    trip = await d.link.request('read_trip', { trip_id: ctx.tripId });
  } catch {
    return { refused: refusalWords('server_down', vague).text };
  }
  const decision = trip.decisions.find((x) => x.decision_id === o.decisionId);
  if (!decision || decision.kind !== 'choice') return { refused: refusalWords('no_vote', vague).text };
  if (decision.status !== 'closed') return { refused: refusalWords('decision_not_closed', vague).text };

  // The choice is the winner, or, after a tie, one of the options that tied at the top.
  const top = Math.max(0, ...decision.tally);
  const winner = decision.result_index ?? null;
  const allowed = winner !== null ? o.choiceIndex === winner : top > 0 && decision.tally[o.choiceIndex] === top;
  const winnerLabel = winner !== null ? (decision.options[winner]?.label ?? 'the winner') : 'one of the options that tied';
  const chosen = decision.options[o.choiceIndex];
  if (!allowed || !chosen) return { refused: `The group picked ${winnerLabel}. I can only set that.` };

  const payer = trip.people.find((p) => p.person_id === o.payerPersonId);
  if (!payer) return { refused: "That person isn't in this trip." };

  // The cap is never invented: the price plus a small margin, or an amount someone wrote in the chat.
  const said = amountsSaid(ctx.pack.messages, trip.people.length);
  const price = chosen.price_minor ?? null;
  const currency = chosen.currency ?? trip.money.currency;
  if (price !== null && o.capMinor < price) return { refused: `That cap is below the price of ${chosen.label} (${money(price, currency)}).` };
  const withinMargin = price !== null && o.capMinor <= marginCap(price);
  if (!withinMargin && !said.includes(o.capMinor)) {
    const most = price !== null ? `, up to ${money(marginCap(price), currency)},` : '';
    return { refused: `Tell me the cap the group agreed on. I can use the price plus a little margin${most} or a number someone said.` };
  }

  const m = decision.mandate;
  if (m && m.choice_index === o.choiceIndex && m.cap_minor === o.capMinor && m.currency === currency && m.refundable_only === o.refundableOnly && m.payer_person_id === payer.person_id) {
    return { refused: `That's already set. Waiting on ${payer.name} to tap Yes, book it.` };
  }

  await d.link.request('set_mandate', {
    request_id: ctx.stableId('go_mandate'),
    trip_id: ctx.tripId,
    decision_id: o.decisionId,
    choice_index: o.choiceIndex,
    cap_minor: o.capMinor,
    currency,
    refundable_only: o.refundableOnly,
    payer_person_id: payer.person_id,
  });
  const cardId = ctx.stableId('c_mandate');
  ctx.cards.push(
    mandateCard({ cardId, decisionId: o.decisionId, what: chosen.label, capMinor: o.capMinor, currency, refundableOnly: o.refundableOnly, payerName: payer.name }),
  );
  return { cardId };
}
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/money/tools.ts
import { z } from 'zod';
import type { QuickTool, TurnCtx } from '../composer/tools.js';
import type { AskResult } from './ask.js';

/**
 * Only the vote, the winning option as the vote names it, and the purpose. There is no payer and no card here: the payer
 * comes from the mandate. option_ref is the vote option's own reference (an options card's option_id), not a travel offer id.
 */
export const AskToPayInput = z.strictObject({
  decision_id: z.string().min(1).max(64),
  option_ref: z.string().min(1).max(200),
  purpose: z.string().min(1).max(200),
});
export type AskToPayInput = z.infer<typeof AskToPayInput>;

export const SetMandateInput = z.strictObject({
  decision_id: z.string().min(1).max(64),
  choice_index: z.int().nonnegative(),
  cap_minor: z.int().positive(),
  refundable_only: z.boolean(),
  payer_person_id: z.string().min(1).max(64),
});
export type SetMandateInput = z.infer<typeof SetMandateInput>;

type AskRun = (ctx: TurnCtx, o: { decisionId: string; offerId: string; purpose: string }) => Promise<AskResult>;
type SetRun = (
  ctx: TurnCtx,
  o: { decisionId: string; choiceIndex: number; capMinor: number; refundableOnly: boolean; payerPersonId: string },
) => Promise<{ cardId: string } | { refused: string }>;

export function askToPayTool(run: AskRun): QuickTool<AskToPayInput> {
  return {
    name: 'ask_to_pay',
    description:
      "Ask the payer to approve paying for what the group voted for. Works only after the vote closed, the mandate is set and the payer tapped Yes, book it; option_ref is the winning option's ref from the vote, exactly as Payments in right now gives it. It checks the live price, gets the Server's approval and puts a pay card the payer approves with Face ID. You never pay, never ask for card details and never offer another way to pay. If it refuses, say why in one short line.",
    input: AskToPayInput,
    async run(args, ctx) {
      // Pay.askToPay's offerId (master §6.3's name) carries the vote option's option_ref; askToPay resolves the offer.
      const r = await run(ctx, { decisionId: args.decision_id, offerId: args.option_ref, purpose: args.purpose });
      if ('cardId' in r) return { pay_card: r.cardId, tell_the_room: 'Say in one short line that the pay card is up for the payer.' };
      return { refused: r.refused, tell_the_room: 'Say this in your own short words. Never offer another way to pay.' };
    },
  };
}

export function setMandateTool(run: SetRun): QuickTool<SetMandateInput> {
  return {
    name: 'set_mandate',
    description:
      'After a choice vote closes, record what the group allows: the winning option, who pays (the organizer or the person the group named in the chat), whether only refundable rates are allowed, and the most to spend in paise: the price plus a small margin, or a number someone in the group said. Never invent a cap or a payer. The payer then taps Yes, book it on the card.',
    input: SetMandateInput,
    async run(args, ctx) {
      const r = await run(ctx, {
        decisionId: args.decision_id,
        choiceIndex: args.choice_index,
        capMinor: args.cap_minor,
        refundableOnly: args.refundable_only,
        payerPersonId: args.payer_person_id,
      });
      return 'cardId' in r ? { mandate_card: r.cardId, tell_the_room: 'Say in one short line what was set and that the payer taps Yes, book it.' } : { refused: r.refused };
    },
  };
}
```

```ts
// apps/brain/src/money/__tests__/kit.ts
import { randomBytes } from 'node:crypto';
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import * as C from '@go/contract';
import Fastify from 'fastify';
import type pg from 'pg';
import type { z } from 'zod';
import { makeTurnCtx, type QuickTool, type TurnCtx } from '../../composer/tools.js';
import { withSchema } from '../../db/test-db.js';
import { TO_SERVER_SCHEMAS, type Message, type ToServerMethod, type ToServerParams, type ToServerResult, type Trip } from '../../door/methods.js';
import type { ServerLink } from '../../door/server-link.js';
import type { Confirmed, Hold, Travel } from '../../travel/travel.js';
import { askToPay, idsFrom, postCard, type AskResult } from '../ask.js';
import type { MoneyDeps } from '../deps.js';
import type { PayLinkInput, PayLinkRefusal, VaultClient } from '../vault-client.js';

/** The contract's spend approval test key (TEST KEY ONLY), so the fake Server signs real approvals. */
export const SPEND_VECTOR = JSON.parse(
  readFileSync(new URL('../../../../../packages/contract/test-vectors/spend-approval.json', import.meta.url), 'utf8'),
) as { private_key_pem: string; public_key_pem: string };

/** An ask for the villa vote. offerId carries the winning option's option_ref (master's name for Pay.askToPay). */
export const VILLA = { decisionId: 'd_villa', offerId: 'opt_villa_sol', purpose: 'Villa Sol, 3 nights, 6 to 9 Nov' };

const PEOPLE: Array<[string, string, 'organizer' | 'member']> = [
  ['p_rahul', 'Rahul', 'organizer'],
  ['p_priya', 'Priya', 'member'],
  ['p_arjun', 'Arjun', 'member'],
  ['p_meher', 'Meher', 'member'],
  ['p_kabir', 'Kabir', 'member'],
];

export type VillaStage = 'open' | 'closed' | 'mandate' | 'confirmed' | 'declined';

/** The guide's Goa trip, cut down to the stay vote, at a chosen stage. Villa Sol wins 4 to 1; Rahul pays. */
export function goaTrip(stage: VillaStage = 'confirmed', o: { capMinor?: number; refundableOnly?: boolean } = {}): Trip {
  const closed = stage !== 'open';
  const mandate =
    stage === 'mandate' || stage === 'confirmed' || stage === 'declined'
      ? {
          choice_index: 0,
          cap_minor: o.capMinor ?? 5500000,
          currency: 'INR',
          refundable_only: o.refundableOnly ?? true,
          payer_person_id: 'p_rahul',
          set_at: '2026-10-05T08:46:00Z',
          ...(stage === 'confirmed' || stage === 'declined' ? { confirmed_at: '2026-10-05T08:58:00Z' } : {}),
          ...(stage === 'declined' ? { declined_at: '2026-10-05T08:59:00Z' } : {}),
        }
      : null;
  return C.TripSnapshot.parse({
    trip_id: 't_goa',
    seq: 100,
    title: 'Goa',
    status: 'planning',
    created_at: '2026-10-05T02:30:00Z',
    organizer_person_id: 'p_rahul',
    invite_url: 'https://go.example/j/8fK2xQ',
    vibe: [],
    budget: { answered: 0, of: 5 },
    people: PEOPLE.map(([person_id, name, role]) => ({ person_id, name, role, joined_at: '2026-10-05T02:30:00Z', know: [], has_upi: false, go_paused: false })),
    decisions: [
      {
        decision_id: 'd_villa',
        question: 'Where do we stay?',
        kind: 'choice',
        anonymous: false,
        status: closed ? 'closed' : 'open',
        options: [
          // option_ref is the options card's option_id (P4's put_to_vote), not a travel offer id.
          { label: 'Villa Sol', price_minor: 5420000, currency: 'INR', option_ref: 'opt_villa_sol', note: '3 nights, private pool, Anjuna' },
          { label: 'Casa Azul', price_minor: 6180000, currency: 'INR', option_ref: 'opt_casa_azul' },
          { label: 'The Palms', price_minor: 5850000, currency: 'INR', option_ref: 'opt_the_palms' },
        ],
        voted: 5,
        of: 5,
        tally: [4, 1, 0],
        ...(closed ? { result_index: 0, closed_at: '2026-10-05T08:45:00Z' } : {}),
        ...(mandate ? { mandate } : {}),
        opened_at: '2026-10-05T07:30:00Z',
      },
    ],
    bookings: [],
    expenses: [],
    money: { currency: 'INR', spent_minor: 0, by_category: [], transfers: [] },
    itinerary: [],
    record: [],
  });
}

/** A person's chat message, for the "amounts said" rule. */
export function say(seq: number, personId: string, text: string): Message {
  return C.Message.parse({
    message_id: `m_${seq}`,
    seq,
    updated_seq: seq,
    author: { kind: 'person', person_id: personId },
    text,
    attachments: [],
    mentions_go: false,
    mentions: [],
    reactions: [],
    withheld: false,
    deleted: false,
    sent_at: '2026-10-05T08:50:00Z',
  });
}

/** A Server answer that is an error frame, the way P1's ServerLink rejects (a numeric `code`). */
export class FakeCallError extends Error {
  constructor(
    readonly code: number,
    message: string,
  ) {
    super(message);
  }
}

interface ApprovalRow {
  approval_id: string;
  trip_id: string;
  decision_id: string;
  payer_person_id: string;
  amount_minor: number;
  currency: string;
  exp: number;
  used: boolean;
  cancelled: boolean;
  cancel_reason: string | null;
}

/**
 * The Server, for money tests: answers Go's requests from one trip, runs the guide's spend check, signs
 * approvals with the test key, checks approvals on add_booking, and records every write once per request_id.
 */
export class FakeServerLink implements ServerLink {
  readonly trips = new Map<string, Trip>();
  readonly writes: Array<{ method: ToServerMethod; params: Record<string, unknown> }> = [];
  readonly approvals = new Map<string, ApprovalRow>();
  readonly cards = new Map<string, C.Card>();
  readonly posts: Array<{ message_id: string; text: string | null; card: C.Card | null }> = [];
  readonly failNext = new Map<ToServerMethod, Error>();
  private readonly answers = new Map<string, unknown>();
  private n = 0;

  constructor(
    trip: Trip,
    private readonly now: () => Date,
    private readonly spendKeyPem: string = SPEND_VECTOR.private_key_pem,
  ) {
    this.trips.set(trip.trip_id, trip);
  }

  isOpen(): boolean {
    return true;
  }

  notify(): void {}

  trip(tripId: string): Trip {
    const trip = this.trips.get(tripId);
    if (!trip) throw new FakeCallError(404, `no trip ${tripId}`);
    return trip;
  }

  writesOf(method: ToServerMethod): Array<{ method: ToServerMethod; params: Record<string, unknown> }> {
    return this.writes.filter((w) => w.method === method);
  }

  async request<M extends ToServerMethod>(method: M, params: ToServerParams<M>): Promise<ToServerResult<M>> {
    const [paramsSchema, resultSchema] = TO_SERVER_SCHEMAS[method as ToServerMethod] as unknown as readonly [z.ZodType, z.ZodType | null];
    const p = paramsSchema.parse(params) as Record<string, unknown>;
    const failure = this.failNext.get(method);
    if (failure) {
      this.failNext.delete(method);
      throw failure;
    }
    const key = typeof p.request_id === 'string' ? `${String(p.trip_id)}|${p.request_id}` : null;
    if (key && this.answers.has(key)) return this.answers.get(key) as ToServerResult<M>;
    this.writes.push({ method, params: p });
    const out = this.handle(method, p);
    const checked = resultSchema ? resultSchema.parse(out) : undefined;
    if (key) this.answers.set(key, checked);
    return checked as ToServerResult<M>;
  }

  private seq(trip: Trip): number {
    trip.seq += 1;
    return trip.seq;
  }

  private handle(method: ToServerMethod, p: Record<string, unknown>): unknown {
    const trip = this.trip(String(p.trip_id));
    switch (method) {
      case 'read_trip':
        return structuredClone(trip);
      case 'post_message': {
        const card = (p.card ?? null) as C.Card | null;
        if (card && this.cards.has(card.card_id)) throw new FakeCallError(409, 'card_id already in this room');
        if (card?.kind === 'mandate' && !trip.decisions.find((x) => x.decision_id === card.decision_id)?.mandate) throw new FakeCallError(409, 'the vote has no mandate');
        const message_id = `m_${++this.n}`;
        if (card) this.cards.set(card.card_id, card);
        this.posts.push({ message_id, text: typeof p.text === 'string' ? p.text : null, card });
        return { message_id, seq: this.seq(trip) };
      }
      case 'replace_card': {
        const card = p.card as C.Card;
        if (!this.cards.has(card.card_id)) throw new FakeCallError(404, 'no such card');
        this.cards.set(card.card_id, card);
        return { message_id: 'm_card' };
      }
      case 'set_mandate': {
        const decision = trip.decisions.find((x) => x.decision_id === p.decision_id);
        if (!decision) throw new FakeCallError(404, 'no such vote');
        if (decision.kind !== 'choice' || decision.status !== 'closed') throw new FakeCallError(409, 'not a closed choice vote');
        decision.mandate = {
          choice_index: Number(p.choice_index),
          cap_minor: Number(p.cap_minor),
          currency: String(p.currency),
          refundable_only: Boolean(p.refundable_only),
          payer_person_id: String(p.payer_person_id),
          set_at: this.now().toISOString(),
        };
        return { seq: this.seq(trip) };
      }
      case 'request_spend_approval':
        return this.spend(trip, p);
      case 'cancel_spend_approval': {
        const row = this.approvals.get(String(p.approval_id));
        if (!row || row.trip_id !== trip.trip_id) throw new FakeCallError(404, 'no such approval');
        if (!row.used) {
          row.cancelled = true;
          row.cancel_reason = String(p.reason);
        }
        return { ok: true };
      }
      case 'add_booking': {
        if (typeof p.approval_id === 'string') {
          const row = this.approvals.get(p.approval_id);
          if (!row || row.trip_id !== trip.trip_id) throw new FakeCallError(409, "the approval is not one of this trip's");
          if (p.decision_id !== row.decision_id) throw new FakeCallError(409, 'the approval is for another vote');
          if (row.used) throw new FakeCallError(409, 'the approval is already used');
          if (row.cancelled) throw new FakeCallError(409, 'the approval was cancelled');
          if (p.currency !== row.currency || Number(p.amount_minor) > row.amount_minor) throw new FakeCallError(409, 'the booking costs more than the approval allows');
          row.used = true;
        }
        const booking_id = `b_${++this.n}`;
        trip.bookings.push(C.Booking.parse({ ...p, booking_id, status: 'confirmed', created_at: this.now().toISOString() }));
        return { booking_id, seq: this.seq(trip) };
      }
      case 'add_expense': {
        const expense_id = `e_${++this.n}`;
        trip.expenses.push(C.Expense.parse({ ...p, expense_id, created_at: this.now().toISOString() }));
        return { expense_id, seq: this.seq(trip) };
      }
      default:
        throw new FakeCallError(-32601, `${method} is not played by the money kit`);
    }
  }

  /** The guide's spend check, in its order, then a signed one-use approval. */
  private spend(trip: Trip, p: Record<string, unknown>): unknown {
    const now = this.now();
    const decision = trip.decisions.find((x) => x.decision_id === p.decision_id);
    const m = decision?.mandate;
    const outstanding = [...this.approvals.values()].some(
      (a) => a.trip_id === trip.trip_id && a.decision_id === p.decision_id && !a.used && !a.cancelled && a.exp * 1000 > now.getTime(),
    );
    const deny = (reason: string) => ({ status: 'denied', reason });
    if (!decision) return deny('decision_not_found');
    if (decision.status !== 'closed') return deny('decision_not_closed');
    if (decision.kind !== 'choice' || !m) return deny('no_mandate');
    if (m.declined_at && (!m.confirmed_at || Date.parse(m.declined_at) > Date.parse(m.confirmed_at))) return deny('payer_declined');
    if (!m.confirmed_at) return deny('payer_has_not_confirmed');
    if (p.currency !== m.currency) return deny('currency_mismatch');
    if (Number(p.amount_minor) > m.cap_minor) return deny('over_cap');
    if (m.refundable_only && !(typeof p.refundable_until === 'string' && Date.parse(p.refundable_until) > now.getTime())) return deny('not_refundable');
    if (outstanding) return deny('approval_outstanding');
    const iat = Math.floor(now.getTime() / 1000);
    const exp = iat + C.MAX_APPROVAL_TTL_SECONDS;
    const approval_id = `ap_${randomBytes(6).toString('hex')}`;
    const token = C.signSpendApproval(
      {
        iss: 'server',
        aud: 'go-vault',
        jti: approval_id,
        trip_id: trip.trip_id,
        decision_id: decision.decision_id,
        payer_person_id: m.payer_person_id,
        amount_minor: Number(p.amount_minor),
        currency: String(p.currency),
        merchant: String(p.merchant),
        purpose: String(p.purpose),
        refundable_until: typeof p.refundable_until === 'string' ? p.refundable_until : null,
        iat,
        exp,
      },
      this.spendKeyPem,
    );
    this.approvals.set(approval_id, {
      approval_id,
      trip_id: trip.trip_id,
      decision_id: decision.decision_id,
      payer_person_id: m.payer_person_id,
      amount_minor: Number(p.amount_minor),
      currency: String(p.currency),
      exp,
      used: false,
      cancelled: false,
      cancel_reason: null,
    });
    return { status: 'approved', approval_id, approval: token, expires_at: new Date(exp * 1000).toISOString() };
  }
}

export interface FakeOffer {
  amountMinor: number;
  currency: string;
  merchant: string;
  refundableUntil: string | null;
  title: string;
  kind: C.Booking['kind'];
}

/**
 * The travel layer for money tests: options cards' option ids that stand for offers (null for one a job found by
 * browsing), holds with a live price, and confirms once per hold.
 */
export class FakeTravel implements Travel {
  /** Each options-card option, by its option_id (a vote option's option_ref), and the travel offer it stands for. */
  readonly options = new Map<string, string | null>([
    ['opt_villa_sol', 'ixigo:stay:villa-sol-anjuna'],
    ['opt_casa_azul', 'ixigo:stay:casa-azul-assagao'],
    ['opt_the_palms', 'ixigo:stay:the-palms-vagator'],
  ]);
  /** Live prices, by travel offer id. */
  readonly offers = new Map<string, FakeOffer>([
    [
      'ixigo:stay:villa-sol-anjuna',
      { amountMinor: 5420000, currency: 'INR', merchant: 'ixigo: Villa Sol, Anjuna', refundableUntil: '2026-11-01T18:29:59Z', title: 'Villa Sol, 3 nights', kind: 'stay' },
    ],
  ]);
  /** Every option id offer() was asked about, in order. */
  readonly looked: string[] = [];
  readonly holds: Array<{ offerId: string; lead: { name: string; personIds: string[] }; holdId: string }> = [];
  readonly confirms: Array<{ holdId: string; paid: { approvalId: string; railRef: string } }> = [];
  failHold = false;
  failConfirm = false;
  private readonly confirmed = new Map<string, Confirmed>();

  constructor(private readonly now: () => Date) {}

  /** The live price of a travel offer. */
  priced(offerId: string): FakeOffer {
    const o = this.offers.get(offerId);
    if (!o) throw new Error(`no offer ${offerId}`);
    return o;
  }

  price(offerId: string, amountMinor: number): void {
    this.offers.set(offerId, { ...this.priced(offerId), amountMinor });
  }

  async offer(optionId: string): Promise<{ offerId: string | null; title: string; priceMinor: number | null; currency: string | null } | null> {
    this.looked.push(optionId);
    if (!this.options.has(optionId)) return null;
    const offerId = this.options.get(optionId) ?? null;
    const o = offerId ? this.offers.get(offerId) : undefined;
    return { offerId, title: o?.title ?? optionId, priceMinor: o?.amountMinor ?? null, currency: o?.currency ?? null };
  }

  async search(): Promise<never> {
    throw new Error('search is not used by the money tests');
  }

  async status(): Promise<never> {
    throw new Error('status is not used by the money tests');
  }

  async hold(offerId: string, lead: { name: string; personIds: string[] }): Promise<Hold> {
    if (this.failHold) throw new Error('the hold failed');
    const o = this.priced(offerId);
    const holdId = `hold_${this.holds.length + 1}`;
    this.holds.push({ offerId, lead, holdId });
    const hold = {
      holdId,
      amountMinor: o.amountMinor,
      currency: o.currency,
      merchant: o.merchant,
      expiresAt: new Date(this.now().getTime() + 15 * 60_000).toISOString(),
      payment: { kind: 'fake' as const },
      ...(o.refundableUntil ? { refundableUntil: o.refundableUntil } : {}),
    };
    return hold;
  }

  async confirm(holdId: string, paid: { approvalId: string; railRef: string }): Promise<Confirmed> {
    const done = this.confirmed.get(holdId);
    if (done) return done;
    this.confirms.push({ holdId, paid });
    if (this.failConfirm) throw new Error('ixigo did not confirm');
    const held = this.holds.find((h) => h.holdId === holdId);
    const o = this.priced(held?.offerId ?? 'ixigo:stay:villa-sol-anjuna');
    const result: Confirmed = {
      ref: 'IXG-7Q2K9',
      vendor: 'ixigo',
      title: o.title,
      kind: o.kind,
      startsAt: '2026-11-06T14:00:00+05:30',
      endsAt: '2026-11-09T11:00:00+05:30',
      ...(o.refundableUntil ? { refundableUntil: o.refundableUntil } : {}),
      place: { name: 'Villa Sol', address: 'Anjuna, North Goa' },
    };
    this.confirmed.set(holdId, result);
    return result;
  }
}

/** The vault for unit tests: records pay links and answers what the test says. */
export class FakeVaultClient implements VaultClient {
  readonly payLinks: PayLinkInput[] = [];
  readonly homeLinks: Array<{ personId: string; start: string }> = [];
  refuse: PayLinkRefusal | null = null;
  hang = false;
  charged: string[] = [];
  withoutBothKeysValue = 0;

  constructor(private readonly now: () => Date) {}

  async homeLink(i: { personId: string; start: 'home' | 'add_card' | 'add_login' }): Promise<{ url: string; expiresAt: string }> {
    this.homeLinks.push(i);
    return { url: `https://vault.test/l/home_${this.homeLinks.length}`, expiresAt: new Date(this.now().getTime() + 300_000).toISOString() };
  }

  async payLink(i: PayLinkInput, o?: { timeoutMs?: number }): Promise<{ ok: true; url: string; expiresAt: string } | { ok: false; reason: PayLinkRefusal }> {
    if (this.hang) {
      await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, o?.timeoutMs ?? 2_000));
      return { ok: false, reason: 'unavailable' };
    }
    if (this.refuse) return { ok: false, reason: this.refuse };
    this.payLinks.push(i);
    return { ok: true, url: `https://vault.test/p/pay_${this.payLinks.length}`, expiresAt: new Date(this.now().getTime() + 300_000).toISOString() };
  }

  async bothKeys(): Promise<{ chargedApprovalIds: string[]; withoutBothKeys: number }> {
    return { chargedApprovalIds: this.charged, withoutBothKeys: this.withoutBothKeysValue };
  }
}

export interface MoneyKit {
  pool: pg.Pool;
  drop: () => Promise<void>;
  server: FakeServerLink;
  travel: FakeTravel;
  vault: FakeVaultClient;
  deps: MoneyDeps;
  lines: string[];
  now(): Date;
  setNow(at: Date): void;
  advance(ms: number): void;
  /** askToPay outside a turn, with ids from `trigger` (each call gets its own unless given). */
  ask(o?: { decisionId: string; offerId: string; purpose: string }, trigger?: string): Promise<AskResult>;
}

/** A fresh schema, the fake Server with the Goa trip, fake travel and vault, and a clock at 09:00 on 5 Oct. */
export async function moneyKit(o: { trip?: Trip; at?: Date } = {}): Promise<MoneyKit> {
  const { pool, drop } = await withSchema();
  let now = o.at ?? new Date('2026-10-05T09:00:00Z');
  const clock = () => now;
  const server = new FakeServerLink(o.trip ?? goaTrip('confirmed'), clock);
  const travel = new FakeTravel(clock);
  const vault = new FakeVaultClient(clock);
  const lines: string[] = [];
  const log = Fastify({ logger: { level: 'trace', stream: { write: (l: string) => void lines.push(l) } } }).log;
  const deps: MoneyDeps = { pool, link: server, travel: () => travel, vault, payRail: () => 'fake', now: clock, log, tapVaultMs: 200 };
  let n = 0;
  return {
    pool,
    drop,
    server,
    travel,
    vault,
    deps,
    lines,
    now: clock,
    setNow: (at) => {
      now = at;
    },
    advance: (ms) => {
      now = new Date(now.getTime() + ms);
    },
    ask: (a = VILLA, trigger = `trigger_${++n}`) => {
      const ids = idsFrom(trigger, a.decisionId);
      return askToPay(deps, 't_goa', a, ids, postCard(deps, 't_goa', ids.card));
    },
  };
}

/** A composer turn's context, made by P1's own makeTurnCtx (turn id "turn_test"). */
export function turnCtx(tripId: string, link: ServerLink, trip: Trip, messages: Message[] = []): TurnCtx {
  return makeTurnCtx({ tripId, turnId: 'turn_test', link, pack: { trip, messages, seq: trip.seq }, wake: [], signal: new AbortController().signal });
}

/** Runs tool calls the way the composer does: by name, input checked by the tool's own schema. */
export async function runToolCalls(tools: Array<QuickTool<any>>, ctx: TurnCtx, calls: Array<{ name: string; input: unknown }>): Promise<unknown[]> {
  const out: unknown[] = [];
  for (const call of calls) {
    const tool = tools.find((t) => t.name === call.name);
    if (!tool) {
      out.push({ error: `no tool named ${call.name}` });
      continue;
    }
    const args = tool.input.safeParse(call.input);
    out.push(args.success ? await tool.run(args.data, ctx) : { error: 'the input did not match the tool' });
  }
  return out;
}
```

### Task 12: The pay card's buttons, and the trip changes money listens to

Approve answers `open` with the vault page, only for the payer, inside P1's 2.5 s tap limit: the stored pay link when it has time left, a fresh one when it does not. An approval or hold that ran out turns the card expired and starts a fresh ask after the answer is sent, with ids taken from the tap, so a repeated tap does nothing twice. Decline releases the approval and turns the card declined. When the payer taps "Yes, book it", Go asks to pay straight away, with no model turn in between; a "Not now", a new mandate or a cancelled vote closes any pay card still waiting.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/brain/src/money/taps.ts`
- Create: `apps/brain/src/money/events.ts`
- Test: `apps/brain/src/money/__tests__/taps.test.ts`
- Test: `apps/brain/src/money/__tests__/events.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `CardTapParams`, `CardTapResult`, `TripEvent` (P1 `door/methods.ts`); `Card` from `@go/contract`; `MoneyDeps`, `postLine`, `Later` (Task 11); `askOutsideTurn`, `AskResult` (Task 11); `rid` (Task 9); `byApproval`, `openForDecision`, `patchPayment`, `transition`, `PaymentRow` (Task 9); `PaymentKind`, `PayLinkInput` (Task 9); `TOASTS` (Task 10); the kit (Task 11).
- Produces:
  - `interface TapDeps { d: MoneyDeps; ask: (tripId, o, trigger) => Promise<AskResult>; later: Later; reconcile: (paymentId: string) => Promise<void> }`.
  - `approveTap(t, tap, card) => Promise<CardTapResult>`; `declineTap(t, tap, card) => Promise<CardTapResult>`.
  - `tripEvent(t, ev: TripEvent) => Promise<void>`.

```ts
    expect(await approveTap(t, tap('p_rahul'), card)).toEqual({ result: 'open', url: 'https://vault.test/p/pay_1', expires_at: '2026-10-05T09:05:00.000Z' });

});
```
